⑥ The bank put the screws on him to pay back the loan by the end of the month.
= 银行向他施压，要他在月底前还清贷款。
put the screws on somebody = 对某人施加压力
⑦ I completely screwed up the job interview by arriving an hour late and forgetting my notes.
= 我晚到了一个小时，还忘了带笔记，工作面试彻底搞砸了。
screw up = 搞砸
⑧ She screwed up her eyes against the bright sunlight and looked out to sea.
= 她在刺眼的阳光下眯起眼睛，望向大海。
screw your eyes up = 眯起眼睛
⑨ The baby screwed up her face and began to cry when she tasted the lemon.
= 婴儿尝到柠檬，皱起小脸哭了起来。
screw your face up = 皱起脸
⑩ Stop screwing around in the garden and come and help me carry these boxes upstairs.
= 别在花园里瞎混了，过来帮我把这些箱子搬上楼。
screw around = 瞎混、胡闹
⑪ Wine bottles with a screw cap are easier to open than those with a cork.
= 螺旋盖的葡萄酒瓶比软木塞的好开。
a screw cap = 螺旋盖
⑫ Make sure the screw top of the jar is closed, or the jam will dry out.
= 一定要把罐子的螺旋盖拧好，不然果酱会干掉。
a screw top = 螺旋盖

screwball
/ˈskruːbɔːl/
核心：名词，可数（北美口语）：怪人、古怪的人；（棒球）螺旋球。形容词：古怪的、荒诞滑稽的：screwball comedy 是（二十世纪三四十年代的）神经喜剧。
① My uncle is a lovable screwball who wears a different hat every day.
= 我叔叔是个可爱的怪人，每天戴一顶不同的帽子。
a screwball = 怪人
② We watched an old screwball comedy in which a rich girl falls in love with a poor reporter.
= 我们看了一部老神经喜剧，讲一个富家女爱上了一个穷记者。
screwball comedy = 神经喜剧

screwdriver
/ˈskruːdraɪvə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：螺丝刀、改锥；（饮料）伏特加橙汁鸡尾酒。
① I need a small screwdriver to open the back of the remote control and change the batteries.
= 我需要一把小螺丝刀，打开遥控器后盖换电池。
a screwdriver = 螺丝刀
② She ordered a screwdriver at the bar and sat down by the window.
= 她在吧台点了一杯伏特加橙汁，在窗边坐下。
a screwdriver = 伏特加橙汁鸡尾酒

screwed-up
/ˌskruːd ˈʌp/
核心：形容词（口语）：心理有问题的、心理扭曲的：a screwed-up childhood；（纸）揉成一团的；（面部）皱起的。
① He had a screwed-up childhood, with parents who were always fighting and never at home.
= 他的童年过得一团糟，父母总在吵架，还从不着家。
a screwed-up childhood = 一团糟的童年
screwed-up = 心理有问题的
② The floor of his room was covered in screwed-up pieces of paper.
= 他房间的地上满是揉成团的纸。
screwed-up paper = 揉成团的纸

screwy
/ˈskruːi/
核心：形容词（尤北美口语）：古怪的、荒唐的、不正常的。比较级 screwier。
① There is something screwy about this bill; we did not order three bottles of wine.
= 这张账单有点不对劲，我们根本没点三瓶葡萄酒。
something screwy = 有点不对劲
screwy = 古怪的
② He had some screwy idea about building a house made entirely of bottles.
= 他有个荒唐的念头，想用瓶子盖一整座房子。
a screwy idea = 荒唐的念头

scribble
/ˈskrɪbl/
核心：动词：潦草地写：scribble a note；乱涂乱画。名词：潦草的字迹、涂鸦（不可数或可数）。名词 scribbler 另立条。
① She scribbled a quick note to her husband and left it on the kitchen table.
= 她匆匆给丈夫写了张便条，留在厨房桌上。
scribble a note = 匆匆写张便条
scribble = 潦草地写
② My little son scribbled all over the wall with his crayons while I was on the phone.
= 我打电话的时候，小儿子用蜡笔在墙上到处乱画。
scribble on something = 在某物上乱画
③ I could not read his scribble, so I asked him to type the letter instead.
= 我认不出他潦草的字，就请他把信打出来。
somebody's scribble = 某人潦草的字迹

scribbler
/ˈskrɪblə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（幽默或贬义）：（蹩脚的）作家、写手、文人。
① He called himself a humble scribbler, although his books had sold millions of copies.
= 虽然他的书卖了几百万册，他还是自称是个卑微的写手。
a scribbler = 写手
② The politician dismissed the journalists as a bunch of scribblers who knew nothing.
= 那位政客把记者们斥为一帮什么都不懂的蹩脚文人。
a bunch of scribblers = 一帮蹩脚文人

scribe
/skraɪb/
核心：名词，可数：（印刷术发明以前的）抄写员、书记员；（幽默）作家、记者。
① In the Middle Ages, monks worked as scribes, copying books by hand.
= 在中世纪，修士们当抄写员，用手抄书。
scribes = 抄写员
a scribe = 抄写员
② The old sports scribe has written about football for the local paper for forty years.
= 那位老体育记者为当地报纸写足球写了四十年。
a sports scribe = 体育记者

scrimmage
/ˈskrɪmɪdʒ/
核心：名词，可数：混战、扭打；（美式橄榄球）争球；（北美）（训练中的）队内练习赛。动词：混战、争抢。
① There was a scrimmage at the door as everyone tried to get out of the hot room at the same time.
= 大家同时想从闷热的房间里出去，门口挤成一团。
a scrimmage = 混战、扭打
② The coach divided the players into two teams for a scrimmage at the end of practice.
= 训练结束时，教练把球员分成两队打一场队内练习赛。
a scrimmage = 队内练习赛

scrimp
/skrɪmp/
核心：动词，不及物：节省、省吃俭用：scrimp and save 是省吃俭用、精打细算。
① My parents scrimped and saved for years so that we could go to a good university.
= 我父母省吃俭用了好多年，好让我们能上一所好大学。
scrimp and save = 省吃俭用
scrimp = 节省
② Don't scrimp on the shoes; cheap ones will hurt your feet on a long walk.
= 鞋子上别省钱，便宜的鞋走远路会磨脚。
scrimp on something = 在某物上省钱

scrip
/skrɪp/
核心：名词，不可数或可数：临时凭证、代价券（代替现金使用的票据）；（金融）临时股票、股权证。
① During the war, the workers were paid in scrip that could only be spent at the company shop.
= 战争期间，工人们领的是代价券，只能在公司的商店里花。
be paid in scrip = 领代价券
scrip = 代价券
② Shareholders can choose to receive new shares as scrip instead of a cash payment.
= 股东可以选择以股票代替现金分红。
receive scrip = 领取股票

script
/skrɪpt/
核心：名词：（电影、戏剧、广播的）剧本、脚本（可数）；文字、字体（可数或不可数）：Arabic script；手写体（不可数）；（计算机）脚本（可数）。动词：为……写剧本；（比喻）事先安排好：a scripted answer。复合词 script kiddie 是（贬义）脚本小子：用别人写好的程序搞网络破坏的外行。
① The actors sat round a big table and read through the script together before they started filming.
= 开拍前，演员们围坐在一张大桌子旁，一起把剧本过了一遍。
a script = 剧本
read through the script = 通读剧本
② The old manuscript in the library is written in a beautiful Arabic script with gold letters.
= 图书馆里那份古老的手稿是用漂亮的阿拉伯文字写成的，还带着金字。
Arabic script = 阿拉伯文字
③ The programmer wrote a short script that sends a report to everyone in the team each morning.
= 那位程序员写了一个小脚本，每天早上给团队里的每个人发一份报告。
a script = （计算机）脚本
④ The police said the attack on the website was the work of a teenage script kiddie, not a criminal gang.
= 警方说，攻击那家网站的是一个十几岁的脚本小子，而不是犯罪团伙。
a script kiddie = 脚本小子

scripted
/ˈskrɪptɪd/
核心：形容词：照稿子念的、事先写好的：a scripted speech；（节目）有剧本的（与即兴相对）。
① The president's answers sounded scripted, as if someone else had written them for him.
= 总统的回答听起来像是照稿子念的，好像是别人替他写的。
scripted = 照稿子念的
② Most of the comedy on the show is scripted, but some of the best jokes are made up on the spot.
= 那档节目的大部分喜剧都有剧本，可有些最好笑的段子是现场编的。
be scripted = 有剧本

scriptorium
/skrɪpˈtɔːriəm/
核心：名词，可数，复数 scriptoria 或 scriptoriums：（中世纪修道院的）缮写室：修士抄写书籍的房间。
① In the scriptorium, monks copied books by hand by the light of the windows.
= 在缮写室里，修士们借着窗户的光手抄书籍。
the scriptorium = 缮写室
② The museum has rebuilt a medieval scriptorium with desks, inks and old pens.
= 那座博物馆复建了一间中世纪缮写室，有书桌、墨水和旧笔。
a medieval scriptorium = 中世纪缮写室

scriptural
/ˈskrɪptʃərəl/
核心：形容词：圣经的、经文的、宗教经典的。
① On Sunday the priest used several scriptural passages to explain his ideas about forgiveness.
= 星期天，神父引用了几段经文来阐述他对宽恕的看法。
scriptural passages = 经文段落
scriptural = 经文的
② There is no scriptural basis for some of the rules that the old church followed.
= 那个老教会遵守的一些规矩在经典里找不到依据。
a scriptural basis = 经典依据

scripture
/ˈskrɪptʃə(r)/
核心：名词，不可数或可数：（基督教）圣经（也作 Scripture、the Scriptures）；（其他宗教的）经文、经典。
① The old man read a passage of scripture aloud to his family every evening.
= 那位老人每天晚上都给家人朗读一段圣经。
scripture = 圣经
a passage of scripture = 一段经文
② Students of religion study the Hindu scriptures as well as the Bible and the Koran.
= 宗教学的学生除了圣经和古兰经，也学习印度教经典。
the Hindu scriptures = 印度教经典

scriptwriter
/ˈskrɪptraɪtə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（电影、电视、广播的）编剧、撰稿人。
① She worked as a scriptwriter for a popular television comedy for ten years.
= 她给一部受欢迎的电视喜剧当了十年编剧。
a scriptwriter = 编剧
② The scriptwriters went on strike, and many shows had to stop filming.
= 编剧们罢工了，许多节目只好停拍。
the scriptwriters = 编剧们

scrofula
/ˈskrɒfjələ/
核心：名词，不可数（医学，旧称）：瘰疬：颈部淋巴结的结核病。
① In the Middle Ages, people believed that the king's touch could cure scrofula.
= 中世纪的人相信国王触摸一下就能治好瘰疬。
scrofula = 瘰疬
② The old medical book describes scrofula as a swelling of the glands in the neck.
= 那本旧医书把瘰疬描述为颈部腺体肿大。
describe scrofula = 描述瘰疬

scroll
/skrəʊl/
核心：名词，可数：卷轴、卷册：古时写字用的长卷；（建筑、家具上的）涡卷形装饰。动词：（在电脑、手机上）滚动、翻屏：scroll down / through something。复合词 scroll bar 是滚动条。
① The museum has an ancient scroll that is more than two thousand years old.
= 那座博物馆有一卷两千多年前的古代卷轴。
a scroll = 卷轴
② She scrolled through the photos on her phone, looking for the one of her grandmother.
= 她在手机上翻着照片，找祖母的那张。
scroll through something = 翻看某物
scroll = 滚动、翻屏
③ Scroll down to the bottom of the page to find the address and phone number.
= 滚动到页面底部，就能找到地址和电话号码。
scroll down = 往下滚动
④ Drag the scroll bar on the right to move through the long document more quickly.
= 拖动右边的滚动条，能更快地翻阅这份长文档。
the scroll bar = 滚动条

Scrooge
/skruːdʒ/
核心：名词，可数（贬义）：守财奴、吝啬鬼：来自狄更斯小说《圣诞颂歌》中吝啬的老头 Ebenezer Scrooge。也常小写。
① Don't be such a Scrooge; buy your friends a drink for once.
= 别那么抠门，就请朋友们喝一次酒吧。
a Scrooge = 吝啬鬼
② My boss is a real Scrooge who complains every time we use the office printer.
= 我老板是个真正的守财奴，我们每用一次办公室的打印机他都要抱怨。
a real Scrooge = 真正的守财奴

scrotum
/ˈskrəʊtəm/
核心：名词，可数，复数 scrota 或 scrotums（解剖）：阴囊。
① The doctor examined a small lump on the boy's scrotum and said it was nothing serious.
= 医生检查了男孩阴囊上的一个小肿块，说没什么大问题。
the scrotum = 阴囊
② Doctors advise men to check their scrotum regularly for any lumps or changes.
= 医生建议男性定期检查阴囊有无肿块或变化。
check the scrotum = 检查阴囊

scrounge
/skraʊndʒ/
核心：动词（口语，常含贬义）：白要、蹭（钱、东西）：scrounge something off / from somebody；四处搜罗。
① He is always scrounging cigarettes off his friends because he never buys his own.
= 他总蹭朋友的烟，因为他从不自己买。
scrounge something off somebody = 向某人蹭某物
scrounge = 白要、蹭
② We scrounged some old wood from the building site and made a table for the garden.
= 我们从建筑工地搜罗了些旧木头，给花园做了张桌子。
scrounge something from somewhere = 从某处搜罗某物

scrounger
/ˈskraʊndʒə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（贬义）：蹭吃蹭喝的人、爱占便宜的人。
① My cousin is a terrible scrounger who never brings anything when he comes to dinner.
= 我表哥是个爱蹭吃蹭喝的人，来吃饭从不带任何东西。
a scrounger = 爱蹭吃蹭喝的人
② Some newspapers unfairly called people who received help from the state scroungers.
= 有些报纸不公正地把领政府救济的人称作吃白食的。
scroungers = 吃白食的

scrub
/skrʌb/
核心：动词：（用刷子、粗布）用力擦洗、刷洗：scrub the floor；（口语）取消：scrub a plan。名词：擦洗（单数）：give something a scrub；灌木丛、矮树林（不可数）；磨砂膏（可数）。和 wipe 的分别：wipe 是用布轻轻抹，scrub 是使劲刷洗。
短语动词：scrub off 是擦掉；scrub out 是把（锅、柜子）里面刷干净；scrub up 是（外科医生）手术前洗手。复合词：scrub nurse 是器械护士，scrub room 是（手术室旁的）洗手间。
① She scrubbed the kitchen floor on her hands and knees until it was clean.
= 她跪在地上用力擦洗厨房地板，直到擦干净。
scrub = 用力刷洗（要使劲）
scrub = 用力擦洗
近义对照：
wipe = 擦（轻轻抹）
② The pan was so dirty that I had to give it a good scrub with hot soapy water.
= 那口锅脏得要命，我只好用热肥皂水好好刷了一遍。
give something a scrub = 把某物刷一遍
③ The hills behind the town are covered in dry scrub and a few small trees.
= 小镇后面的山上长满了干枯的灌木丛和几棵小树。
scrub = 灌木丛
④ It took me an hour to scrub off the paint the children had put on the wall.
= 我花了一个小时才把孩子们涂在墙上的颜料擦掉。
scrub off = 擦掉
⑤ Scrub out the cupboards before you put the clean plates back inside.
= 把干净盘子放回去之前，先把柜子里面刷干净。
scrub out = 把……里面刷干净
⑥ The surgeon scrubbed up carefully for five minutes before the operation began.
= 手术开始前，外科医生仔细洗了五分钟的手。
scrub up = （手术前）洗手
⑦ The scrub nurse handed the surgeon each instrument as he needed it.
= 器械护士在外科医生需要时把每件器械递给他。
a scrub nurse = 器械护士
⑧ The doctors washed their hands and put on clean gowns in the scrub room next to the theatre.
= 医生们在手术室隔壁的洗手间里洗手、换上干净的手术衣。
the scrub room = （手术室旁的）洗手间

scrubber
/ˈskrʌbə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（洗锅的）擦洗刷、钢丝球；（工业）（烟气的）洗涤器、净化器。另有英式俚语一义，是对女性的侮辱性称呼，这一义不配例句。
① I need a new scrubber for the burnt pan, because the old one has fallen apart.
= 我得给烧焦的锅换一个新的擦洗刷，旧的已经散了。
a scrubber = 擦洗刷
② The power station has fitted scrubbers to remove dangerous gases from its chimneys.
= 那座发电站装了洗涤器，清除烟囱里的有害气体。
scrubbers = （烟气）洗涤器

scrubby
/ˈskrʌbi/
核心：形容词：长满矮树丛的、灌木丛生的；（树木）矮小的、发育不良的。
① We walked for miles across scrubby land where only a few goats could find food.
= 我们徒步穿过几英里灌木丛生的荒地，那里只有几只山羊能找到吃的。
scrubby land = 灌木丛生的土地
scrubby = 灌木丛生的
② A few scrubby trees grew along the dry river bed at the bottom of the valley.
= 山谷底部干涸的河床边长着几棵矮小的树。
scrubby trees = 矮小的树

scrubland
/ˈskrʌblænd/
核心：名词，不可数：灌木丛林地、灌木地带：只长矮树和灌木的干旱土地。
① The national park includes large areas of scrubland where wild horses live.
= 那座国家公园包括大片灌木丛林地，野马生活在那里。
scrubland = 灌木丛林地
② The fire spread quickly through the dry scrubland and reached the edge of the town.
= 大火迅速蔓延过干燥的灌木地带，烧到了小镇边上。
dry scrubland = 干燥的灌木地带

scruff
/skrʌf/
核心：名词：the scruff of the neck 是后颈、颈背：抓 / 揪住后颈；（英式口语）邋遢的人（可数）。
① The mother cat picked up her kitten by the scruff of its neck and carried it to safety.
= 母猫叼着小猫的后颈，把它衔到了安全的地方。
the scruff of the neck = 后颈
② The teacher grabbed the boy by the scruff of the neck and pulled him away from the fight.
= 老师一把揪住那个男孩的后脖领，把他从打架中拉开。
grab somebody by the scruff of the neck = 揪住某人的后脖领
③ He looked like a real scruff in his torn jeans and dirty old jumper.
= 他穿着破牛仔裤和脏兮兮的旧毛衣，看起来真是个邋遢鬼。
a scruff = （英式口语）邋遢鬼

scruffiness
/ˈskrʌfinəs/
核心：名词，不可数：邋遢、不整洁。由 scruffy 变 y 为 i 加 -ness 构成。
① His mother complained about the scruffiness of his clothes and bought him a new suit.
= 他妈妈抱怨他衣服邋遢，给他买了一套新西装。
the scruffiness of something = 某物的邋遢
scruffiness = 邋遢
② Despite the scruffiness of the old café, the food was the best in town.
= 尽管那家老咖啡馆不太整洁，饭菜却是镇上最好的。
despite the scruffiness = 尽管不整洁

scruffy
/ˈskrʌfi/
核心：形容词：邋遢的、不整洁的、破旧的：scruffy clothes、a scruffy little hotel。比较级 scruffier。副词 scruffily 是邋遢地。名词 scruffiness 另立条。
① He turned up for the interview in scruffy jeans and an old T-shirt, so he did not get the job.
= 他穿着邋遢的牛仔裤和旧 T 恤去面试，所以没得到那份工作。
scruffy clothes = 邋遢的衣服
scruffy = 邋遢的
② We stayed in a scruffy little hotel near the station because it was all we could afford.
= 我们住在车站附近一家破旧的小旅馆，因为只住得起那儿。
a scruffy hotel = 破旧的旅馆
③ The students were scruffily dressed, but they were polite and very clever.
= 那些学生穿得邋里邋遢，但很有礼貌，也很聪明。
scruffily dressed = 穿得邋里邋遢

scrum
/skrʌm/
核心：名词，可数：（英式橄榄球）并列争球：双方前锋抱成一团争球；（口语）（人群的）推搡拥挤：a scrum of reporters。复合词 scrum half 是（橄榄球）传锋：把球送进并列争球、再传出的队员。
① The referee called a scrum after one of the players dropped the ball.
= 一名球员掉球后，裁判判了一次并列争球。
a scrum = 并列争球
② There was a scrum of photographers outside the hotel, all waiting for the film star.
= 酒店外面挤满了摄影师，都在等那位影星。
a scrum of reporters = 挤成一团的记者
③ The scrum half picked up the ball and passed it quickly to the winger.
= 传锋捡起球，迅速传给了边锋。
the scrum half = （橄榄球）传锋

scrummage
/ˈskrʌmɪdʒ/
核心：名词，可数（正式）：（橄榄球）并列争球：scrum 的全称。动词：（橄榄球）进行并列争球。
① The forwards practised the scrummage for an hour before the big match.
= 大赛前，前锋们练了一个小时的并列争球。
the scrummage = 并列争球
② The two packs scrummaged near the line, pushing with all their strength.
= 两队前锋在底线附近并列争球，使出全身力气往前推。
scrummage = 进行并列争球

scrummy
/ˈskrʌmi/
核心：形容词（英式口语）：好吃的、美味的。比较级 scrummier。
① My grandmother made a scrummy chocolate cake with fresh cream for my tenth birthday.
= 我十岁生日时，祖母做了一个配鲜奶油的好吃的巧克力蛋糕。
a scrummy cake = 好吃的蛋糕
scrummy = 好吃的
② These little lemon biscuits are absolutely scrummy, so please have another one.
= 这些小柠檬饼干好吃极了，请再吃一块。
absolutely scrummy = 好吃极了

scrumptious
/ˈskrʌmpʃəs/
核心：形容词（口语）：（食物）美味的、可口的。比 delicious 更口语、带亲切感。
① The hotel served a scrumptious breakfast of fresh bread, eggs and homemade jam.
= 那家酒店的早餐很可口，有新鲜面包、鸡蛋和自制果酱。
a scrumptious breakfast = 可口的早餐
scrumptious = 美味的
② The strawberries from the farm were so scrumptious that we ate the whole basket.
= 农场的草莓太好吃了，我们把一整篮都吃光了。
so scrumptious = 那么美味

scrumpy
/ˈskrʌmpi/
核心：名词，不可数（英式）：（英格兰西南部的）农家烈性苹果酒：未经过滤、浑浊、酒劲大。
① We drank a pint of scrumpy at a little farm in Somerset and felt very sleepy afterwards.
= 我们在萨默塞特的一个小农场喝了一品脱农家苹果酒，之后觉得很困。
a pint of scrumpy = 一品脱农家苹果酒
scrumpy = 农家烈性苹果酒
② Be careful with the scrumpy; it is much stronger than the cider you buy in shops.
= 那农家苹果酒你小心点，比商店里买的苹果酒烈多了。
the scrumpy = 农家苹果酒

scrunch
/skrʌntʃ/
核心：动词：把……揉成一团：scrunch something up；（脚下）发出嘎吱声；皱起（脸）：scrunch up your face。名词，单数：嘎吱声。复合词 scrunch-dry 是（用手抓着）把头发吹出卷曲效果。
① She scrunched up the letter and threw it into the fire without reading it.
= 她看都没看就把信揉成一团扔进了火里。
scrunch something up = 把某物揉成一团
scrunch = 揉成一团
② The snow scrunched under our boots as we walked through the silent forest.
= 我们走过寂静的森林，雪在靴子底下嘎吱作响。
snow scrunches = 雪嘎吱作响
③ Scrunch-dry your hair with your fingers if you want it to look curly and natural.
= 想让头发看起来卷曲自然，就用手指抓着吹干。
scrunch-dry = 用手抓着吹出卷发

scrunchy
/ˈskrʌntʃi/
核心：名词，可数，复数 scrunchies：（布包的）发圈、头绳。也拼作 scrunchie。形容词：（踩上去）嘎吱作响的。
① She tied her hair back with a bright pink scrunchy before she went running.
= 她出门跑步前，用一个亮粉色发圈把头发扎了起来。
a scrunchy = 发圈
② We walked along the scrunchy gravel path to the front door of the old house.
= 我们沿着踩上去嘎吱响的碎石路走到老房子的前门。
scrunchy gravel = 嘎吱作响的碎石

scruple
/ˈskruːpl/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：顾忌、顾虑（道德上的）：have no scruples about doing something、without scruple。动词：（正式，多用否定）有所顾忌：not scruple to do something。
① He has no scruples about lying to his customers if it means he can sell more cars.
= 只要能多卖几辆车，他对欺骗顾客毫无顾忌。
have no scruples about doing something = 做某事毫无顾忌
scruples = 顾忌
② She would not scruple to take the money from her own family if she needed it.
= 要是需要钱，她会毫无顾忌地拿自己家人的钱。
not scruple to do something = 毫无顾忌地做某事

scrupulous
/ˈskruːpjələs/
核心：形容词：一丝不苟的、极仔细的：scrupulous attention to detail；（道德上）严谨正直的、讲良心的。反义 unscrupulous。副词 scrupulously 是一丝不苟地；极其（干净、诚实）：scrupulously clean。名词 scrupulousness 另立条。
① The museum staff take scrupulous care of the old paintings and check them every day.
= 博物馆员工一丝不苟地照料那些古画，每天都检查。
scrupulous care = 一丝不苟的照料
scrupulous = 一丝不苟的
② He was scrupulous about paying back every penny that he had ever borrowed.
= 他借过的每一分钱都一丝不苟地还清。
be scrupulous about something = 在某事上一丝不苟
③ The kitchen of the restaurant was scrupulously clean, with not a crumb on the floor.
= 那家餐馆的厨房干净极了，地上连一点碎屑都没有。
scrupulously clean = 极其干净

scrupulousness
/ˈskruːpjələsnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：一丝不苟、严谨；（道德上的）正直。由 scrupulous 加 -ness 构成。
① Everyone admired the scrupulousness with which she checked every figure in the report.
= 大家都佩服她核对报告里每一个数字时的一丝不苟。
scrupulousness = 一丝不苟
② His scrupulousness in money matters made him the obvious choice to look after the club's accounts.
= 他在钱的事上一向严谨，自然成了管俱乐部账目的不二人选。
scrupulousness in money matters = 在钱的事上的严谨

scrutineer
/ˌskruːtəˈnɪə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（英式）：（选举的）监票员、检票员：检查投票是否公正的人。
① The scrutineers watched carefully as the votes were counted in the town hall.
= 市政厅里点票时，监票员们仔细地看着。
scrutineers = 监票员
② Each party is allowed to send a scrutineer to check that the election is fair.
= 每个政党都可以派一名监票员，检查选举是否公正。
send a scrutineer = 派监票员

scrutinize
/ˈskruːtənaɪz/
核心：动词，及物：仔细检查、细看、审查：scrutinize a document、scrutinize somebody's face。英式也拼作 scrutinise。名词 scrutiny 另立条。
① The lawyer scrutinized the contract carefully before he let his client sign it.
= 律师仔细审查了合同，才让他的委托人签字。
scrutinize something = 仔细审查某物
scrutinize = 仔细检查
② She scrutinized his face, trying to see whether he was telling the truth.
= 她仔细端详他的脸，想看出他是不是在说实话。
scrutinize somebody's face = 端详某人的脸

scrutiny
/ˈskruːtəni/
核心：名词，不可数：仔细检查、审查：come under scrutiny、close scrutiny、public scrutiny。
① The government's plans will come under close scrutiny when parliament discusses them next week.
= 下周议会讨论时，政府的计划将受到严格审查。
come under scrutiny = 受到审查
close scrutiny = 严格审查
② Famous actors and singers have to live their private lives under constant public scrutiny.
= 有名的演员和歌手不得不在公众的持续关注下过私生活。
public scrutiny = 公众监督
③ On closer scrutiny, the painting turned out to be a clever copy.
= 仔细一查，那幅画原来是一件精巧的仿品。
on closer scrutiny = 仔细一查

scud
/skʌd/
核心：动词，不及物（文学）：（云、船）飞驰、疾行：clouds scudding across the sky。
① Dark clouds scudded across the sky as the wind grew stronger before the storm.
= 暴风雨来临前风越刮越大，乌云在天上飞驰。
clouds scud = 云飞驰
scud = 飞驰、疾行
② The little sailing boat scudded across the bay in the strong afternoon wind.
= 下午风大，那条小帆船在海湾里疾驶而过。
scud across something = 疾驶过某处

scuff
/skʌf/
核心：动词：（鞋、地板等）磨损、刮擦：scuff your shoes；拖着脚走。名词，可数：磨痕、刮痕。形容词 scuffed 是磨损的（另立条）。
① On their first day, the children scuffed their new shoes playing football in the playground.
= 第一天上学，孩子们就在操场上踢足球，把新鞋磨坏了。
scuff your shoes = 把鞋磨坏
scuff = 磨损
② There were black scuffs on the white floor where people had dragged the chairs.
= 白色地板上有人拖过椅子的地方留下了黑色的刮痕。
scuffs = 磨痕

scuffed
/skʌft/
核心：形容词：（鞋、地板、家具）磨损的、有刮痕的。
① His shoes were scuffed and dirty after the long walk through the woods.
= 在树林里走了很长的路后，他的鞋又磨损又脏。
scuffed shoes = 磨损的鞋
scuffed = 磨损的
② The old piano was badly scuffed, but it still had a beautiful sound.
= 那架旧钢琴刮痕累累，可音色仍然很美。
badly scuffed = 刮痕累累

scuffle
/ˈskʌfl/
核心：名词，可数：扭打、混战：a scuffle broke out。动词，不及物：扭打、混战：scuffle with somebody。比 fight 规模小、时间短。
① A scuffle broke out between two fans outside the stadium, but the police stopped it quickly.
= 体育场外两名球迷扭打起来，不过警察很快就制止了。
a scuffle broke out = 发生扭打
a scuffle = 扭打
② Protesters scuffled with police as they tried to get into the building.
= 抗议者想冲进大楼时，与警察发生了推搡扭打。
scuffle with somebody = 与某人扭打

scuffling
/ˈskʌflɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：扭打；（小动物发出的）窸窣声、刮擦声。
① We heard a scuffling in the roof at night and realized that mice had got in.
= 夜里我们听到屋顶上有窸窣声，才知道老鼠钻进去了。
a scuffling = 窸窣声
② There was some scuffling at the back of the hall, and two men were asked to leave.
= 礼堂后面发生了一些推搡，有两个人被请了出去。
some scuffling = 一些推搡扭打

scull
/skʌl/
核心：名词，可数：（双桨划艇用的）短桨；单人（或双人）双桨赛艇。动词：用短桨划（船）。
① He rowed the little boat across the lake with a pair of light wooden sculls.
= 他用一对轻便的木制短桨把小船划过了湖。
a pair of sculls = 一对短桨
② Every Sunday she sculled slowly down the river in the early morning mist.
= 每个星期天清晨的薄雾里，她都划着双桨赛艇顺河慢慢而下。
scull down a river = 划桨顺河而下

sculler
/ˈskʌlə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：双桨赛艇运动员、划双桨船的人。
① The young sculler trained on the river every morning at six before going to school.
= 那位年轻的双桨赛艇运动员每天早上六点上学前都在河上训练。
a sculler = 双桨赛艇运动员
② She became the fastest sculler in the country and won a gold medal at the Olympics.
= 她成了全国最快的双桨赛艇手，还在奥运会上拿了金牌。
the fastest sculler = 最快的双桨赛艇手

scullery
/ˈskʌləri/
核心：名词，可数：（旧时大宅厨房旁的）洗涤间：洗碗、洗菜的小房间。
① In the old house, the maids washed the dishes in a cold scullery next to the kitchen.
= 在那座老宅里，女仆们在厨房旁边冰冷的洗涤间里洗碗。
a scullery = 洗涤间
② We turned the old scullery into a small laundry room with a washing machine.
= 我们把旧洗涤间改成了放洗衣机的小洗衣房。
turn a scullery into something = 把洗涤间改成某物

sculling
/ˈskʌlɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：双桨划船运动、单人双桨赛艇。
① Sculling is harder than it looks, because you have to use both arms at exactly the same time.
= 双桨划船比看上去难，因为两只胳膊得完全同时用力。
sculling = 双桨划船
② She took up sculling at university and now races for her local club.
= 她上大学时开始玩双桨赛艇，现在代表当地俱乐部比赛。
take up sculling = 开始玩双桨赛艇

sculpt
/skʌlpt/
核心：动词：雕刻、雕塑：sculpt a figure in stone；（比喻）塑造、使成形。
① The artist sculpted a beautiful horse out of a single block of white stone.
= 那位艺术家用一整块白石雕刻出了一匹漂亮的马。
sculpt something out of something = 用某物雕出某物
sculpt = 雕刻
② The wind and waves have sculpted the cliffs into strange shapes over thousands of years.
= 几千年来，风浪把悬崖塑造成了奇形怪状。
sculpt something into something = 把某物塑造成某形状

sculptor
/ˈskʌlptə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：雕塑家、雕刻家。
① The sculptor spent three years working on the huge statue in the city square.
= 那位雕塑家花了三年时间创作城市广场上的那座巨大雕像。
a sculptor = 雕塑家
② The young sculptor works mainly with metal and old pieces of machinery.
= 那位年轻雕塑家主要用金属和旧机器零件创作。
work as a sculptor = 当雕塑家

sculptress
/ˈskʌlptrəs/
核心：名词，可数（旧式）：女雕塑家。今天一般不分性别，都说 sculptor。
① The museum has a whole room of works by a famous sculptress from the nineteenth century.
= 那座博物馆有一整间展厅陈列一位十九世纪著名女雕塑家的作品。
a sculptress = 女雕塑家
② She was one of the first women to be called a sculptress in an age when few women made art.
= 在很少有女性从事艺术的年代，她是最早被称为女雕塑家的人之一。
be called a sculptress = 被称为女雕塑家

sculptural
/ˈskʌlptʃərəl/
核心：形容词：雕塑的、雕塑般的：sculptural forms。
① The new building has a sculptural shape, with curved white walls that look like waves.
= 那座新楼造型如雕塑一般，弯曲的白墙像波浪。
a sculptural shape = 雕塑般的造型
sculptural = 雕塑般的
② The modern garden behind the gallery is full of sculptural plants with large dramatic leaves.
= 美术馆后面那个现代花园里满是叶子硕大、造型夸张的雕塑感植物。
sculptural plants = 雕塑感植物

sculpture
/ˈskʌlptʃə(r)/
核心：名词：雕塑作品、雕像（可数或不可数）；雕塑艺术（不可数）。动词：雕刻。人是 sculptor（雕塑家）。
① The park is full of modern sculptures made of stone, metal and glass.
= 公园里满是用石头、金属和玻璃做的现代雕塑作品。
sculpture = 雕塑
sculptures = 雕塑作品
② She studied sculpture at art college before she became a famous painter.
= 她成为名画家之前，在美术学院学的是雕塑。
study sculpture = 学雕塑
③ The sculptor who made the statue in the square also made the one in front of the station.
= 广场上那座雕像的雕塑家，也做了车站前面那座。
sculptor = 雕塑家

sculptured
/ˈskʌlptʃəd/
核心：形容词：雕刻的、雕塑装饰的；（面容、身材）线条分明的、轮廓清晰的。
① The church door is decorated with sculptured figures of angels and saints.
= 那座教堂的大门上装饰着天使和圣徒的雕像。
sculptured figures = 雕像
sculptured = 雕刻的
② The young model is famous for her sculptured cheekbones and bright green eyes.
= 那位年轻模特以轮廓分明的颧骨和明亮的绿眼睛出名。
sculptured cheekbones = 轮廓分明的颧骨

scum
/skʌm/
核心：名词，不可数：（液体表面的）浮渣、泡沫：a layer of scum；（俚语，贬义）人渣、败类。
① A layer of green scum covered the surface of the old pond behind the factory.
= 工厂后面那个旧池塘的水面上漂着一层绿色浮渣。
a layer of scum = 一层浮渣
scum = 浮渣
② The people who sold the fake medicine to sick children are the scum of the earth.
= 把假药卖给生病孩子的那些人是社会败类。
the scum of the earth = 社会败类

scumbag
/ˈskʌmbæɡ/
核心：名词，可数（俚语，冒犯）：卑鄙小人、混蛋。
① Some scumbag stole my bike from outside the hospital while I was visiting my mother.
= 我去医院看我妈的时候，不知哪个混蛋从门外偷走了我的自行车。
some scumbag = 某个混蛋
a scumbag = 卑鄙小人
② The detective called the drug dealer a scumbag and threw him into the police car.
= 侦探骂那个毒贩是个人渣，把他扔进了警车。
call somebody a scumbag = 骂某人是人渣

scummy
/ˈskʌmi/
核心：形容词：（水）有浮渣的、肮脏的；（口语）卑鄙的、下流的、令人恶心的。比较级 scummier。
① The water in the old swimming pool was green and scummy, so nobody wanted to go in.
= 那个旧游泳池的水又绿又脏，谁都不想下去。
scummy water = 有浮渣的脏水
scummy = 肮脏的
② It was a scummy thing to do, taking money from a blind old man.
= 拿一个失明老人的钱，这事干得太卑鄙了。
a scummy thing to do = 卑鄙的事

scupper
/ˈskʌpə(r)/
核心：动词，及物（英式口语）：破坏、使（计划）落空：scupper a plan；（故意）凿沉（船）。名词，可数：（船的）排水孔。
① The bad weather scuppered our plans for a barbecue in the garden.
= 坏天气让我们在花园里烧烤的计划泡了汤。
scupper a plan = 使计划落空
scupper = 破坏
② The crew scuppered the ship so that the enemy could not capture it.
= 船员们凿沉了那艘船，免得被敌人缴获。
scupper a ship = 凿沉船
③ Water poured off the deck through the scuppers as the waves crashed over the boat.
= 海浪砸上船时，水从排水孔里哗哗流出甲板。
the scuppers = 排水孔

scurrilous
/ˈskʌrələs/
核心：形容词（正式）：恶语中伤的、诽谤的：scurrilous rumours、a scurrilous attack。副词 scurrilously 是恶意中伤地。
① Last week the newspaper printed scurrilous rumours about the minister's private life and family.
= 上周那家报纸刊登了中伤部长私生活和家人的谣言。
scurrilous rumours = 中伤人的谣言
scurrilous = 恶语中伤的
② He was scurrilously attacked in an anonymous letter that was sent to all his customers.
= 他在一封寄给他所有客户的匿名信里遭到了恶意中伤。
scurrilously = 恶意中伤地

scurry
/ˈskʌri/
核心：动词，不及物：（小步）急匆匆地跑、疾走：scurry away / across。名词，单数：急促的奔跑（声）。
① A mouse scurried across the kitchen floor and disappeared under the cupboard.
= 一只老鼠窜过厨房地板，钻进柜子底下不见了。
scurry across something = 窜过某处
scurry = 急匆匆地跑
② Office workers scurried along the street, trying to get out of the rain.
= 上班族们沿着街道急匆匆地跑，想躲雨。
scurry along = 急匆匆地走

scurvy
/ˈskɜːvi/
核心：名词，不可数：坏血病：长期缺乏维生素 C 引起的疾病，旧时常见于远航的水手。
① In the past, sailors on long voyages often died of scurvy because they had no fresh fruit.
= 过去，远航的水手常常因为没有新鲜水果而死于坏血病。
die of scurvy = 死于坏血病
scurvy = 坏血病
② Captains began to carry limes on their ships to protect the crew from scurvy.
= 船长们开始在船上带酸橙，防止船员得坏血病。
protect somebody from scurvy = 防止某人得坏血病

scuttle
/ˈskʌtl/
核心：动词：（小步）急促地跑：scuttle away；（故意）凿沉（船）；使（计划）失败。名词，可数：煤斗、煤桶：a coal scuttle。
① The crab scuttled sideways across the sand and hid under a rock.
= 那只螃蟹横着窜过沙滩，躲到了一块石头底下。
scuttle across something = 急促地窜过某处
scuttle = 急促地跑
② Strong opposition from local people finally scuttled the plans for a new airport.
= 当地人的强烈反对最终让新建机场的计划泡了汤。
scuttle a plan = 使计划失败
③ My grandmother filled the coal scuttle every morning and carried it to the fire.
= 我祖母每天早上把煤斗装满，提到壁炉边。
a coal scuttle = 煤斗

scuttlebutt
/ˈskʌtlbʌt/
核心：名词，不可数（北美口语）：小道消息、流言。原指船上的饮水桶，水手们在桶边闲聊。
① The scuttlebutt in the office is that the boss is going to retire next year.
= 办公室里的小道消息说老板明年要退休了。
the scuttlebutt = 小道消息
② Don't believe all the scuttlebutt you hear around the school about the new teacher.
= 别全信你在学校里听到的关于那个新老师的流言。
believe the scuttlebutt = 相信小道消息

scuzzy
/ˈskʌzi/
核心：形容词（北美俚语）：肮脏的、邋遢的、令人恶心的。比较级 scuzzier。
① We spent the night in a scuzzy motel with dirty sheets and a broken television.
= 我们在一家肮脏的汽车旅馆过了一夜，床单脏兮兮的，电视也坏了。
a scuzzy motel = 肮脏的汽车旅馆
scuzzy = 肮脏的
② The bar was dark and scuzzy, but the music was the best in town.
= 那家酒吧又暗又邋遢，可音乐是镇上最好的。
dark and scuzzy = 又暗又邋遢

scythe
/saɪð/
核心：名词，可数：长柄大镰刀：割草或收割庄稼用。动词：用大镰刀割；（比喻）（像镰刀一样）横扫、穿过。
① The old farmer cut the long grass in the orchard with a scythe, swinging it slowly from side to side.
= 老农用长柄大镰刀割果园里的长草，慢慢左右挥动。
a scythe = 长柄大镰刀
② In the last minute, the winger scythed through the defence and scored a brilliant goal.
= 最后一分钟，那名边锋像镰刀一样切入防线，打进了一记漂亮的进球。
scythe through something = 横扫穿过某处

sea
/siː/
核心：名词：海、海洋（可数或不可数，常作 the sea）：swim in the sea、by the sea；（专名）……海：the Black Sea；（单数）（海面的）状况：a calm / rough sea；a sea of something 是大量的、一大片。和 ocean 的分别：ocean 指大洋，比 sea 更大。
习语：at sea 是在海上航行（不加 the），也指茫然不知所措；by sea 是走海路；go to sea 是去当水手。
复合词（作定语表示「海上的、海里的、近海的」）：sea level 是海平面；sea lion 是海狮；sea cow 是海牛；sea cucumber 是海参；sea urchin 是海胆；sea turtle 是海龟；sea fish 是海鱼；sea salt 是海盐；sea air 是海边的空气；sea breeze 是海风；sea fret 是（英式）海雾；sea green 是海绿色；sea lane 是航道；sea mile 是海里；sea power 是海上力量、海军强国；sea wall 是防波堤；sea captain 是船长；sea dog 是老水手；sea shanty / sea chantey 是水手号子；sea legs 是（不晕船的）适应能力；sea change 是巨变。
① The children ran across the sand and jumped into the sea, shouting with excitement.
= 孩子们跑过沙滩跳进海里，兴奋地大喊大叫。
sea = 海
泛指大海时说 the sea；作定语表示「海上的、海里的」，构成一大族复合词。
② My grandparents live in a little white house by the sea in Cornwall.
= 我祖父母住在康沃尔海边的一座小白房子里。
by the sea = 在海边
近义对照：
ocean = 大洋（比海更大）
③ The ship had been at sea for three months when the sailors finally saw land.
= 那艘船在海上航行了三个月，水手们才终于看到陆地。
at sea = 在海上（不加 the）
加了 the 的 at the sea 是「在海边」，意思不同。
④ I am completely at sea with this new computer program; can you show me how it works?
= 这个新电脑程序我完全摸不着头脑，你能教我怎么用吗？
be at sea = 茫然不知所措
⑤ The heavy furniture was sent to Australia by sea, while we flew there by plane.
= 笨重的家具走海路运到澳大利亚，我们自己坐飞机去。
by sea = 走海路
⑥ The sea was so rough that half the passengers on the ferry were sick.
= 海浪太大，渡轮上一半的乘客都吐了。
a rough sea = 风浪大的海面
⑦ When I looked out from the stage, I saw a sea of faces staring back at me.
= 我从台上往下看，只见黑压压一片面孔盯着我。
a sea of something = 一大片、大量
⑧ The town is only two metres above sea level, so it often floods in winter.
= 那座小镇只比海平面高两米，所以冬天常发洪水。
sea level = 海平面
说海拔高度用 above sea level，不加冠词。
⑨ A group of sea lions lay on the rocks in the sun, barking at the passing boats.
= 一群海狮躺在岩石上晒太阳，冲着经过的船只叫。
a sea lion = 海狮
⑩ The sea cow feeds on underwater plants in warm shallow water near the coast.
= 海牛在近海温暖的浅水里以水下植物为食。
a sea cow = 海牛
⑪ In the market we saw dried sea cucumber, which is very expensive in some countries.
= 我们在集市上看到了干海参，在有些国家它非常昂贵。
sea cucumber = 海参
⑫ Be careful not to step on a sea urchin when you walk among the rocks.
= 在礁石间走的时候小心别踩到海胆。
a sea urchin = 海胆
⑬ A sea turtle came up onto the beach at night to lay her eggs in the sand.
= 一只海龟夜里爬上海滩，在沙里产卵。
a sea turtle = 海龟
⑭ Most of the sea fish sold in the market were caught that morning by local boats.
= 市场上卖的海鱼大多是当地渔船当天早上捕到的。
sea fish = 海鱼
⑮ Sprinkle a little sea salt on the tomatoes before you serve them.
= 上桌前在番茄上撒一点海盐。
sea salt = 海盐
⑯ The doctor told my grandfather that the fresh sea air would be good for his chest.
= 医生告诉我祖父，新鲜的海边空气对他的肺有好处。
sea air = 海边的空气
⑰ A cool sea breeze blew across the beach in the late afternoon.
= 傍晚时分，凉爽的海风吹过海滩。
a sea breeze = 海风
⑱ A thick sea fret rolled in from the water, and we could not see the end of the pier.
= 浓浓的海雾从海面涌来，我们连码头的尽头都看不见了。
a sea fret = （英式）海雾
⑲ She painted the bedroom walls a soft sea green and put white curtains at the window.
= 她把卧室的墙刷成了柔和的海绿色，窗上挂了白窗帘。
sea green = 海绿色
⑳ The narrow strait is one of the busiest sea lanes in the world.
= 那条狭窄的海峡是世界上最繁忙的航道之一。
a sea lane = 航道
㉑ The little island is about twenty sea miles from the mainland, or two hours by ferry.
= 那座小岛离大陆约二十海里，坐渡轮要两个小时。
a sea mile = 海里
㉒ In the eighteenth century, Britain was the greatest sea power in the world.
= 十八世纪时，英国是世界上最强大的海上强国。
a sea power = 海上强国
㉓ The town built a new sea wall to protect the houses from winter storms.
= 那座小镇修了一道新防波堤，保护房屋免受冬季风暴的侵袭。
a sea wall = 防波堤
㉔ The old sea captain had sailed around the world six times before he retired.
= 那位老船长退休前环游世界航行过六次。
a sea captain = 船长
㉕ My grandfather was an old sea dog who loved to tell stories about storms and pirates.
= 我祖父是个老水手，爱讲风暴和海盗的故事。
an old sea dog = 老水手
㉖ The men in the pub sang a sea shanty about sailors who were far from home.
= 酒馆里的男人们唱了一首关于远离家乡的水手的号子。
a sea shanty = 水手号子
㉗ The children learned an old sea chantey that sailors sang while they pulled the ropes.
= 孩子们学了一首水手拉绳时唱的老号子。
a sea chantey = 水手号子（sea shanty 的另一种说法）
㉘ It took me three days on the boat to get my sea legs, but after that I felt fine.
= 我在船上过了三天才适应了颠簸，之后就没事了。
get your sea legs = 适应海上颠簸
㉙ There has been a sea change in the way people shop since the internet arrived.
= 自从有了互联网，人们的购物方式发生了巨大变化。
a sea change = 巨变
出自莎士比亚，指彻底而深刻的转变，比 a big change 正式。

seabird
/ˈsiːbɜːd/
核心：名词，可数：海鸟：海鸥、海鸠等生活在海边、以鱼为食的鸟。
① Thousands of seabirds nest on the cliffs of the island every spring.
= 每年春天，成千上万只海鸟在那座岛的悬崖上筑巢。
seabirds = 海鸟
② The oil from the damaged ship killed hundreds of seabirds along the coast.
= 那艘受损船只泄漏的石油让沿岸几百只海鸟死亡。
kill seabirds = 使海鸟死亡

seaboard
/ˈsiːbɔːd/
核心：名词，可数（尤北美）：海岸、沿海地区：the eastern seaboard。
① Most of the big cities on the eastern seaboard of the United States were founded by Europeans.
= 美国东海岸的大城市大多是欧洲人建立的。
the eastern seaboard = 东海岸
② The storm hit the whole Atlantic seaboard and closed airports from Boston to Miami.
= 那场风暴袭击了整个大西洋沿岸，从波士顿到迈阿密的机场都关了。
the Atlantic seaboard = 大西洋沿岸

seaborne
/ˈsiːbɔːn/
核心：形容词：海运的、由海上运输的；从海上来的：a seaborne attack。
① Most of the country's oil arrives by seaborne trade from the Middle East.
= 那个国家的石油大多经海运贸易从中东运来。
seaborne trade = 海运贸易
seaborne = 海运的
② The army prepared for a seaborne attack on the beaches at dawn.
= 军队准备在黎明时分从海上进攻海滩。
a seaborne attack = 海上进攻

seafarer
/ˈsiːfeərə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（正式）：海员、航海者。
① The museum tells the story of the seafarers who sailed from this port to the New World.
= 那座博物馆讲述了从这个港口航向新大陆的航海者的故事。
seafarers = 航海者
② Her family had been seafarers for generations, and her brother is still a ship's engineer.
= 她家世代都是海员，她哥哥至今还是一名轮机师。
be seafarers = 当海员

seafaring
/ˈsiːfeərɪŋ/
核心：形容词：航海的、以航海为业的：a seafaring nation。名词，不可数：航海（生活）。
① Britain has a long seafaring history, and many towns on the coast were built on fishing.
= 英国有悠久的航海历史，沿海许多城镇都是靠捕鱼起家的。
seafaring history = 航海历史
seafaring = 航海的
② The old men in the pub told stories of seafaring and life on the ships.
= 酒馆里的老人们讲起航海和船上生活的故事。
stories of seafaring = 航海的故事

seafood
/ˈsiːfuːd/
核心：名词，不可数：海鲜、海产：可以吃的鱼、虾、贝类等。
① The restaurant by the harbour is famous for its fresh seafood, especially the crab.
= 港口边那家餐馆以新鲜海鲜出名，尤其是螃蟹。
seafood = 海鲜
不可数，说一道海鲜菜用 a seafood dish。
② She is allergic to seafood and has to check every dish before she eats it.
= 她对海鲜过敏，每道菜吃之前都得问清楚。
be allergic to seafood = 对海鲜过敏

seafront
/ˈsiːfrʌnt/
核心：名词，可数（尤英式）：（城镇的）海滨区、海滨大道：沿海边的街道和建筑。
① We walked along the seafront after dinner, eating ice cream and watching the boats.
= 晚饭后我们沿着海滨大道散步，吃着冰淇淋看船。
the seafront = 海滨大道
英式海滨度假小镇里的说法，常有步道、商店和游乐场。
② The hotel is right on the seafront, so every room has a view of the bay.
= 那家酒店就在海滨，所以每个房间都能看到海湾。
on the seafront = 在海滨

seagoing
/ˈsiːɡəʊɪŋ/
核心：形容词：（船）适于远航的、远洋的；（人）航海的。
① The company builds large seagoing ships that carry cars around the world.
= 那家公司建造在全世界运送汽车的大型远洋轮船。
seagoing ships = 远洋轮船
seagoing = 远洋的
② The Vikings were a seagoing people who reached North America long before Columbus.
= 维京人是航海民族，远在哥伦布之前就到达了北美。
a seagoing people = 航海民族

seagrass
/ˈsiːɡrɑːs/
核心：名词，不可数：海草：长在浅海海底的草状植物；（编织用的）海草编织物。
① Turtles and fish feed on the seagrass that grows in the shallow bay.
= 海龟和鱼以长在浅海湾里的海草为食。
seagrass = 海草
② We bought a seagrass mat for the kitchen because it is strong and easy to clean.
= 我们给厨房买了一张海草编的垫子，因为它结实又好清洁。
a seagrass mat = 海草编的垫子

seagull
/ˈsiːɡʌl/
核心：名词，可数：海鸥。常简称 gull。
① A seagull grabbed the sandwich out of my hand while I was sitting on the beach.
= 我坐在海滩上的时候，一只海鸥从我手里抢走了三明治。
a seagull = 海鸥
② The seagulls followed the fishing boat back to the harbour, crying loudly.
= 海鸥跟着渔船飞回港口，大声叫着。
seagulls follow a boat = 海鸥追着船飞

seahorse
/ˈsiːhɔːs/
核心：名词，可数：海马：一种头像马、身体直立游动的小鱼，由雄性孵卵。
① We saw a tiny seahorse holding on to a piece of seaweed with its tail.
= 我们看到一只小海马用尾巴缠着一根海藻。
a seahorse = 海马
② The aquarium has a special tank of seahorses that the children love to watch.
= 那个水族馆有一个专门养海马的水箱，孩子们很爱看。
a tank of seahorses = 一箱海马

seal
/siːl/
核心：名词，可数：海豹；印章、图章：the royal seal；封条、封蜡；（防漏的）密封圈、密封层；（比喻）认可：seal of approval。动词：密封、封住：seal an envelope；（用封条）封闭（区域）：seal off；确定、使成定局：seal a deal、seal your fate；seal in 是把（香味、水分）封住。复合词 sealing wax 是封蜡。形容词 sealed 是密封的：a sealed envelope。
① We watched the seals lying on the rocks and swimming in the cold grey water.
= 我们看着海豹躺在岩石上，在冰冷灰暗的海水里游动。
seal = 海豹
② Make sure the lid is tight to seal the jar, or the jam will go bad.
= 一定要把盖子拧紧密封罐子，不然果酱会坏。
seal = 密封
本义是盖印封口，引申为封住、使不透气不漏水。
③ Put the exam papers in a sealed envelope and give them to the head teacher.
= 把试卷放进一个密封信封，交给校长。
sealed envelope = 密封信封
④ The new product has the seal of approval of the national safety council.
= 那款新产品得到了国家安全委员会的认可。
seal of approval = 认可
⑤ The letter had the king's seal on it, so everyone knew it was genuine.
= 那封信上盖着国王的印章，所以谁都知道它是真的。
the king's seal = 国王的印章
⑥ The police sealed off the street after the accident and would not let anyone through.
= 事故发生后，警察封锁了那条街，不让任何人通过。
seal off = 封锁
主语多是警察、军队，宾语是街道、区域、建筑。
⑦ Cook the meat quickly in a hot pan to seal in the juices.
= 在热锅里快速煎肉，把肉汁封在里面。
seal in = 封住（香味、水分）
⑧ The two companies sealed the deal with a handshake and a glass of champagne.
= 两家公司握手碰杯，敲定了那笔交易。
seal a deal = 敲定交易
⑨ In the old days, letters were closed with a drop of red sealing wax.
= 旧时，信件用一滴红色封蜡封口。
sealing wax = 封蜡

sealant
/ˈsiːlənt/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：密封剂、密封胶：涂在缝隙处防水、防漏的物质。
① Put some sealant around the edge of the bath so that water cannot get behind it.
= 在浴缸边缘打一圈密封胶，免得水渗到后面去。
sealant = 密封胶
② The builders used a special sealant to protect the wooden floor from water.
= 工人们用一种特殊的密封剂保护木地板免受水浸。
a special sealant = 特殊的密封剂

sealer
/ˈsiːlə(r)/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：密封剂、封底漆；可数：捕海豹的人或船。
① Paint a coat of sealer on the new wood before you put on the colour.
= 上色之前，先在新木头上刷一层封底漆。
a coat of sealer = 一层封底漆
sealer = 封底漆
② The sealers left the harbour every spring to hunt on the ice.
= 捕海豹的人每年春天离开港口，到冰上去猎捕。
sealers = 捕海豹的人

sealing
/ˈsiːlɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：捕海豹（业）；密封。
① Sealing was once the main industry in the little town, but now most people work in tourism.
= 捕海豹曾是那个小镇的主要产业，可如今大多数人从事旅游业。
sealing = 捕海豹业
② The sealing of the old mine took two years and cost millions of pounds.
= 封闭那座旧矿花了两年时间和几百万英镑。
the sealing of something = 某物的封闭

sealskin
/ˈsiːlskɪn/
核心：名词，不可数：海豹皮。也作定语：sealskin boots。
① The hunters wore warm sealskin boots and coats in the Arctic winter.
= 猎人们在北极的冬天穿着暖和的海豹皮靴和外套。
sealskin boots = 海豹皮靴
sealskin = 海豹皮
② Today the sale of sealskin is banned in many countries because of protests about hunting.
= 由于反对猎杀的抗议，如今许多国家禁止买卖海豹皮。
the sale of sealskin = 买卖海豹皮

seam
/siːm/
核心：名词，可数：（衣服、布料的）接缝、线缝：the seams of a jacket；（两块材料的）接合处；（地质）（煤、矿的）薄层、矿层：a coal seam。习语 be bursting at the seams 是挤得满满的；come / fall apart at the seams 是（计划、组织）崩溃、瓦解。
① The seam on the back of my jacket split when I bent down to pick up the box.
= 我弯腰去搬箱子的时候，外套背后的接缝裂开了。
a seam = 接缝
the seam of a jacket = 外套的接缝
② The miners dug deep under the hills to reach a rich seam of coal.
= 矿工们在山下挖得很深，去开采一层丰富的煤层。
a seam of coal = 煤层
③ The little school is bursting at the seams, with forty children in every classroom.
= 那所小学校挤得满满的，每间教室都有四十个孩子。
be bursting at the seams = 挤得满满的
④ Their plans for the wedding started to fall apart at the seams when the hotel closed.
= 酒店关门后，他们的婚礼计划开始全面崩溃。
fall apart at the seams = 全面崩溃

seaman
/ˈsiːmən/
核心：名词，可数，复数 seamen：海员、水手；（海军）水兵。
① My grandfather was a seaman on a cargo ship and visited ports all over the world.
= 我祖父曾是一艘货轮上的海员，去过世界各地的港口。
a seaman = 海员
② The ship had a crew of twenty seamen and a cook from Liverpool.
= 那艘船有二十名水手和一个来自利物浦的厨师。
a crew of seamen = 一船水手

seamanship
/ˈsiːmənʃɪp/
核心：名词，不可数：航海技术、驾船本领。
① It took great seamanship to bring the small boat safely into the harbour during the storm.
= 在暴风雨中把那条小船安全驶进港口，需要高超的驾船本领。
great seamanship = 高超的航海技术
seamanship = 航海技术
② The young sailors were taught seamanship and navigation on a training ship.
= 年轻的水手们在一艘训练船上学习驾船技术和导航。
teach seamanship = 教航海技术

seamed
/siːmd/
核心：形容词：有接缝的：seamed stockings；（脸）布满皱纹的、沟壑纵横的。
① The old fisherman's face was brown and seamed from years of wind and sun.
= 老渔夫的脸被多年的风吹日晒弄得黝黑，布满了皱纹。
a seamed face = 布满皱纹的脸
seamed = 布满皱纹的
② She wore seamed stockings with a black line running up the back of each leg.
= 她穿着有后缝的长筒袜，每条腿背面都有一道黑线。
seamed stockings = 有后缝的长筒袜

seamless
/ˈsiːmləs/
核心：形容词：无缝的、浑然一体的；（过程）顺畅的、天衣无缝的：a seamless transition。副词 seamlessly 是天衣无缝地、顺畅地。
① The change from the old computer system to the new one was seamless, and nobody noticed any problems.
= 从旧电脑系统换到新系统非常顺畅，谁都没察觉到任何问题。
seamless = 顺畅的
a seamless change = 顺畅的转换
② The new building fits seamlessly into the old street, as if it had always been there.
= 那栋新楼和老街浑然一体，好像一直就在那儿。
seamlessly = 天衣无缝地

seamstress
/ˈsiːmstrəs/
核心：名词，可数：女裁缝、缝纫女工。
① My great-grandmother worked as a seamstress, making dresses for rich women in London.
= 我曾祖母当过女裁缝，给伦敦的有钱太太做衣服。
a seamstress = 女裁缝
② The seamstress took in the waist of my wedding dress and shortened the sleeves.
= 那位女裁缝把我婚纱的腰收小了，袖子也改短了。
a seamstress alters a dress = 女裁缝改衣服

seamy
/ˈsiːmi/
核心：形容词（贬义）：（生活、地区的）阴暗面的、肮脏的、见不得人的：the seamy side of life。比较级 seamier。
① The novel shows the seamy side of life in the city, with drugs, crime and poverty.
= 那部小说展现了城市生活的阴暗面：毒品、犯罪和贫穷。
the seamy side of something = 某事的阴暗面
seamy = 阴暗的
② The detective walked through the seamy streets near the port, looking for the missing girl.
= 侦探走过港口附近那些脏乱的街道，寻找失踪的女孩。
seamy streets = 脏乱的街道

seance
/ˈseɪɒns/
核心：名词，可数：降神会、通灵会：一群人试图与死者灵魂交流的聚会。也写作 séance。
① After her husband died, she went to a seance, hoping to hear from him one last time.
= 丈夫去世后，她去参加了一场通灵会，希望最后一次听到他的消息。
a seance = 通灵会
② In the old film, the guests hold hands around the table during a seance.
= 那部老电影里，客人们在通灵会上围着桌子手拉着手。
hold a seance = 举行通灵会

seaplane
/ˈsiːpleɪn/
核心：名词，可数：水上飞机：能在水面起降的飞机。
① We flew to the island on a little seaplane that landed on the water next to the beach.
= 我们坐一架小型水上飞机去岛上，它就降落在海滩旁边的水面上。
a seaplane = 水上飞机
② The seaplane took off from the lake, sending up a spray of water behind it.
= 水上飞机从湖面起飞，身后溅起一片水花。
the seaplane takes off = 水上飞机起飞

seaport
/ˈsiːpɔːt/
核心：名词，可数：海港、港口城市。
① Rotterdam is one of the largest seaports in the world, with ships arriving every hour.
= 鹿特丹是世界上最大的海港之一，每小时都有船只抵达。
a seaport = 海港
② The old seaport grew rich on trade with Africa and South America.
= 那座古老的港口城市靠与非洲和南美的贸易致富。
the old seaport = 古老的港口城市

sear
/sɪə(r)/
核心：动词：（用高温）烧灼、烤焦；（烹饪）（大火快速）煎封表面：sear the meat；（文学）（感情、记忆）灼痛、深深刻在心里。形容词 searing（另立条）。
① Sear the steak in a very hot pan for one minute on each side.
= 把牛排放进很热的锅里，每面煎一分钟封住表面。
sear the meat = 大火煎封肉
sear = 烧灼、煎封
② The image of the burning house was seared into her memory forever.
= 那座着火的房子的样子永远烙在了她的记忆里。
be seared into your memory = 深深烙在记忆里

search
/sɜːtʃ/
核心：动词：搜查、搜寻（某地、某人）：search a place、search somebody；寻找：search for something；（在电脑上）搜索：search the internet。名词，可数：搜寻、搜查：a search for、in search of；（电脑）搜索。和 search for 的分别：search 后直接接地方或人（被搜的对象），search for 后接要找的东西。
短语动词与复合词：search out 是找出、搜寻到；search engine 是搜索引擎，search engine optimization 是搜索引擎优化；search term 是搜索词；search warrant 是搜查令。
① The police searched the whole house from top to bottom but found nothing at all.
= 警察把整座房子从上到下搜了个遍，却一无所获。
search = 搜查
search a place = 搜查某地
② We searched for the lost ring all evening and finally found it under the sofa.
= 我们找那枚丢了的戒指找了一晚上，最后在沙发底下找到了。
search for = 寻找
search for something = 寻找某物
③ After the earthquake, rescue teams continued their search for survivors through the night.
= 地震后，救援队连夜继续搜寻幸存者。
a search for somebody = 搜寻某人
④ Thousands of young people leave the villages every year in search of work in the cities.
= 每年都有成千上万的年轻人离开农村，到城里找工作。
in search of = 寻找
⑤ Before our trip, I searched the internet for a cheap hotel near the station.
= 出发前，我在网上搜了一家车站附近的便宜旅馆。
search the internet = 上网搜索
⑥ The museum searched out old photographs of the town for its new exhibition.
= 那座博物馆为新展览搜寻到了小镇的老照片。
search out = 找出
⑦ Most people use the same search engine every day without thinking about it.
= 大多数人每天都不假思索地用同一个搜索引擎。
a search engine = 搜索引擎
⑧ The company paid an expert in search engine optimization to bring more visitors to its website.
= 那家公司花钱请了一位搜索引擎优化专家，为网站带来更多访客。
search engine optimization = 搜索引擎优化
⑨ Type a search term into the box and press the button to see the results.
= 在框里输入搜索词，按一下按钮就能看到结果。
a search term = 搜索词
⑩ The police got a search warrant and returned to the flat the next morning.
= 警方拿到了搜查令，第二天早上又回到了那套公寓。
a search warrant = 搜查令

searchable
/ˈsɜːtʃəbl/
核心：形容词：（电子资料）可搜索的、可检索的。
① The library has put all its old newspapers online in a searchable database.
= 那家图书馆把所有旧报纸放到网上，做成了一个可检索的数据库。
a searchable database = 可检索的数据库
searchable = 可搜索的
② All the old letters in the archive are now searchable by name, date and place.
= 档案馆里所有的旧信件现在都可以按姓名、日期和地点检索。
be searchable by something = 可按某项检索

searcher
/ˈsɜːtʃə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：搜寻者、搜查人员；（网上的）搜索者。
① The searchers walked in a long line across the moor, looking for the missing climber.
= 搜寻人员排成长长一排走过荒原，寻找失踪的登山者。
searchers = 搜寻者
② Studies show that most internet searchers only look at the first page of results.
= 研究显示，大多数网上搜索的人只看第一页结果。
internet searchers = 网上搜索的人

searching
/ˈsɜːtʃɪŋ/
核心：形容词：（目光、问题）探究的、刨根问底的、锐利的：a searching look、searching questions。副词 searchingly 是探究地。
① The interviewer asked some searching questions about why I had left my last job.
= 面试官问了几个刨根问底的问题，问我为什么辞掉上一份工作。
searching questions = 刨根问底的问题
searching = 探究的
② She looked at him searchingly, trying to tell whether he was lying.
= 她探究地看着他，想看出他是不是在撒谎。
searchingly = 探究地

searchlight
/ˈsɜːtʃlaɪt/
核心：名词，可数：探照灯。
① During the war, searchlights swept the night sky looking for enemy planes.
= 战争期间，探照灯扫过夜空，搜寻敌机。
searchlights = 探照灯
② The prison guards turned on the searchlight when they heard a noise near the wall.
= 狱警听到墙边有动静，就打开了探照灯。
turn on the searchlight = 打开探照灯

searing
/ˈsɪərɪŋ/
核心：形容词：灼热的：searing heat；（疼痛）剧烈的、灼痛的：a searing pain；（批评）尖锐的、猛烈的。副词 searingly 是灼人地、尖锐地。
① On our holiday in Spain, we stayed indoors during the searing heat of the afternoon.
= 在西班牙度假时，我们在下午酷热难当的时候待在屋里。
searing heat = 灼热
searing = 灼热的
② He felt a searing pain in his leg as he fell off the ladder.
= 他从梯子上摔下来时，腿上一阵剧痛。
a searing pain = 剧痛
③ The book is a searingly honest account of the writer's years in prison.
= 那本书以极其坦诚的笔触记述了作者的牢狱岁月。
searingly honest = 极其坦诚的

seascape
/ˈsiːskeɪp/
核心：名词，可数：海景画；海景。
① The gallery has a room full of seascapes painted by artists who lived in the fishing village.
= 那家画廊有一整间展厅，挂满了住在那个渔村的画家画的海景画。
seascapes = 海景画
② From the top of the cliff we looked out over a wild seascape of rocks and waves.
= 我们从悬崖顶上眺望，只见礁石与波涛交织的一片狂野海景。
a wild seascape = 狂野的海景

seashell
/ˈsiːʃel/
核心：名词，可数：海贝、贝壳。
① The children collected seashells on the beach and put them in a bucket.
= 孩子们在海滩上捡贝壳，放进一个桶里。
seashells = 贝壳
② Hold the big seashell to your ear, and you will hear a sound like the sea.
= 把那只大贝壳放在耳边，你会听到像海浪一样的声音。
a seashell = 海贝

seashore
/ˈsiːʃɔː(r)/
核心：名词，单数（常作 the seashore）：海岸、海滨：海与陆地相接的地方。
① We walked along the seashore at low tide, looking at the crabs in the rock pools.
= 退潮时我们沿着海岸散步，看石潭里的螃蟹。
the seashore = 海岸
② The plants that grow on the seashore must be able to live with salt and strong winds.
= 长在海边的植物必须能经得住盐分和大风。
on the seashore = 在海岸上

seasick
/ˈsiːsɪk/
核心：形容词：晕船的：feel / get seasick。名词 seasickness 另立条。
① I always feel seasick on small boats, so I take tablets before we leave the harbour.
= 我坐小船总会晕船，所以离港前都要吃药。
feel seasick = 晕船
seasick = 晕船的
② Half the passengers got seasick when the ferry sailed into the storm.
= 渡轮驶进暴风雨时，一半乘客晕船了。
get seasick = 晕船

seasickness
/ˈsiːsɪknəs/
核心：名词，不可数：晕船。
① Ginger is said to be good for seasickness, so I always take some on long boat trips.
= 据说生姜对晕船有效，所以坐长途船时我总带一些。
seasickness = 晕船
② The doctor gave me some pills for seasickness before the long sea crossing.
= 长途渡海之前，医生给了我一些晕船药。
pills for seasickness = 晕船药

seaside
/ˈsiːsaɪd/
核心：名词，单数（常作 the seaside，尤英式）：海边、海滨（作为度假的地方）：go to the seaside。作定语：海滨的：a seaside town。复合词 seaside postcard 是（英式）海滨明信片，尤指画面俏皮、带点低俗笑料的那种。
① Every summer when I was a child, we spent two weeks at the seaside.
= 我小时候每年夏天都要在海边待两个星期。
the seaside = 海边
go to the seaside = 去海边
② When they stopped work, my grandparents retired to a quiet seaside town on the south coast.
= 我祖父母退休后，搬到了南部海岸一座安静的海滨小镇。
a seaside town = 海滨小镇
③ My grandfather collected old seaside postcards with silly jokes about fat men and their wives.
= 我祖父收藏旧海滨明信片，上面画着关于胖男人和他们老婆的傻乎乎的笑话。
a seaside postcard = 海滨明信片

season
/ˈsiːzn/
核心：名词，可数：季节：the four seasons；（某活动的）季、期：the rainy season、the holiday season；（体育）赛季：the football season；（电视剧的）一季（北美，英式多说 series）；（演出的）演出季。动词：给（食物）调味：season with salt and pepper。
习语：in season 是（水果、蔬菜）当季的、（动物）发情期的；out of season 是不当季的、淡季的。
复合词：season ticket 是季票、长期票；season finale 是（电视剧）季终集，season premiere 是季播首集。形容词 seasonal（另立条）是季节性的。
① Autumn is my favourite season because of the colours of the leaves.
= 秋天是我最喜欢的季节，因为树叶的颜色好看。
season = 季节
the four seasons = 四季
② In England the football season starts in August and finishes in May, with a short break at Christmas.
= 在英格兰，足球赛季八月开始、五月结束，圣诞节有短暂休赛。
season = 赛季
③ Strawberries are cheap at the market in June because that is when they are in season.
= 草莓六月在市场上便宜，因为那时正当季。
in season = 当季的
④ We went to the island out of season, when the hotels were empty and cheap.
= 我们淡季去了那座岛，那时旅馆都空着，也便宜。
out of season = 淡季、不当季
⑤ The rainy season begins in May, and the roads in the village turn to mud.
= 雨季五月开始，村里的路都变成了烂泥。
the rainy season = 雨季
⑥ Season the soup with salt, pepper and a little lemon juice before you serve it.
= 汤上桌前用盐、胡椒和一点柠檬汁调味。
season something with something = 用某物给某物调味
⑦ My father has had a season ticket for his local football club for thirty years.
= 我父亲买当地足球俱乐部的季票已经三十年了。
a season ticket = 季票
⑧ Millions of people watched the season finale to find out who the killer was.
= 几百万人收看了季终集，想知道凶手是谁。
the season finale = 季终集
⑨ The season premiere of the show will be on television next Sunday evening.
= 那部剧的新一季首集将在下个星期天晚上播出。
the season premiere = 季播首集

seasonable
/ˈsiːznəbl/
核心：形容词：（天气）合时令的、正常的：seasonable weather。反义 unseasonable。
① After a strangely warm December, the weather has become more seasonable, with frost and snow.
= 十二月出奇地暖和之后，天气变得更合时令了，有霜也有雪。
seasonable weather = 合时令的天气
seasonable = 合时令的
② Temperatures this week will be seasonable for the time of year, around ten degrees.
= 本周气温将与这个时节相符，在十度左右。
seasonable for the time of year = 合乎时节

seasonal
/ˈsiːzənl/
核心：形容词：季节性的、随季节变化的：seasonal work、seasonal fruit。副词 seasonally 是季节性地。复合词 seasonal affective disorder（缩写 SAD）是季节性情绪失调：冬季日照少而引起的抑郁。名词 seasonality 另立条。
① Fruit picking is seasonal work, so most of the pickers move to other jobs in winter.
= 摘水果是季节性工作，所以大多数采摘工冬天就换别的活儿了。
seasonal work = 季节性工作
seasonal = 季节性的
② The restaurant changes its menu four times a year to use seasonal vegetables.
= 那家餐馆一年换四次菜单，好用当季的蔬菜。
seasonal vegetables = 当季蔬菜
③ The figures have been seasonally adjusted, so the summer jobs do not make the results look better.
= 这些数据经过了季节性调整，所以夏季工作不会让结果显得更好看。
seasonally adjusted = 经季节性调整的
④ She suffers from seasonal affective disorder and feels very low during the dark winter months.
= 她患有季节性情绪失调，在昏暗的冬季几个月里情绪很低落。
seasonal affective disorder = 季节性情绪失调

seasonality
/ˌsiːzəˈnæləti/
核心：名词，不可数：季节性：随季节而变化的特点。
① The seasonality of tourism means that many hotels in the town close for the whole winter.
= 旅游业的季节性意味着镇上许多旅馆整个冬天都关门。
the seasonality of something = 某事的季节性
seasonality = 季节性
② Farmers have to plan carefully for the seasonality of their income, saving money after the harvest.
= 农民得为收入的季节性波动仔细打算，秋收后要存钱。
the seasonality of income = 收入的季节性

seasoned
/ˈsiːznd/
核心：形容词：经验丰富的、老练的：a seasoned traveller；（食物）调过味的；（木材）风干了的、晾干了的。
① She is a seasoned traveller who has visited more than eighty countries.
= 她是个经验丰富的旅行者，去过八十多个国家。
a seasoned traveller = 经验丰富的旅行者
seasoned = 老练的
② The roast chicken at the little restaurant was well seasoned with garlic and fresh herbs.
= 那家小餐馆的烤鸡用大蒜和新鲜香草调得很入味。
well seasoned = 调味很好的
③ Use seasoned wood for the fire, because wet wood makes too much smoke.
= 生火要用晾干的木柴，湿木头烟太大。
seasoned wood = 晾干的木柴

seasoning
/ˈsiːznɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数或可数：调味品、佐料：盐、胡椒、香草、香料等干调料。和 sauce（湿的酱汁）相对。
① Taste the soup and add more seasoning if you think it needs it.
= 尝尝汤，觉得需要的话再加点调料。
seasoning = 调味品
② The secret of the dish is a seasoning made from dried chillies, garlic and lime.
= 那道菜的秘诀是一种用干辣椒、大蒜和青柠做的调料。
a seasoning made from something = 用某物做的调料

seat
/siːt/
核心：名词，可数：座位、座椅：a window seat、take a seat；（椅子的）座部、坐垫；（议会、委员会的）席位：win a seat；（自行车的）车座；（正式）所在地、中心：the seat of government。动词：使就座：be seated；（场所）可容纳……人：the hall seats 500。
习语：take a seat 是请坐、就座；on the edge of your seat 是紧张兴奋地（看）；in the driving seat 是处于主导地位、掌控局面。
复合词：seat belt 是安全带；seat sale（加拿大）是机票促销。名词 seating 另立条。
① Please take a seat; the doctor will see you in a few minutes.
= 请坐，医生过几分钟就来看您。
take a seat = 就座
② I always ask for a window seat on the plane so that I can see the view.
= 我坐飞机总要靠窗的座位，好看外面的风景。
a window seat = 靠窗的座位
③ Put your seat belt on before we start the car, even if we are only going to the shops.
= 我们发动车子前先系好安全带，哪怕只是去商店。
seat belt = 安全带
④ The small green party won twenty new seats in the election and became the third largest party.
= 那个小绿党在选举中新赢得二十个席位，成了第三大党。
win a seat = 赢得席位
⑤ The film was so exciting that we were on the edge of our seats until the very end.
= 那部电影太紧张刺激了，我们一直看到最后都屏息凝神。
on the edge of your seat = 紧张兴奋地
⑥ Now that she owns most of the company, she is in the driving seat.
= 她现在拥有公司的大部分股份，掌控着局面。
in the driving seat = 处于主导地位
⑦ The captain asked all passengers to remain seated until the plane had stopped completely at the gate.
= 机长请所有乘客保持就座，直到飞机在登机口完全停稳。
remain seated = 保持就座
⑧ The new concert hall by the river seats two thousand people and has a café on the roof.
= 河边那座新音乐厅能容纳两千人，屋顶上还有一家咖啡馆。
seat = 可容纳……人
⑨ The airline is having a seat sale, so flights to Vancouver are half price this month.
= 那家航空公司在搞机票促销，所以这个月飞温哥华的航班半价。
a seat sale = （加拿大）机票促销

seating
/ˈsiːtɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：座位（总称）；座位安排：a seating plan。
① The new stadium outside the city has seating for fifty thousand people and a huge car park.
= 城外那座新体育场有五万个座位，还有一个巨大的停车场。
seating = 座位
seating for people = 可坐多少人的座位
② We spent hours on the seating plan for the wedding, so that no one sat next to someone they disliked.
= 我们花了好几个小时排婚礼的座位，好让谁都不挨着自己讨厌的人坐。
a seating plan = 座位安排

seatmate
/ˈsiːtmeɪt/
核心：名词，可数（北美）：（飞机、火车、教室里的）邻座、同桌。
① My seatmate on the plane talked about his dogs for the whole six hours.
= 我在飞机上的邻座整整六个小时都在讲他的狗。
a seatmate = 邻座
② In her first year at high school she became good friends with her seatmate in the history class.
= 高中第一年，她和历史课上的同桌成了好朋友。
a seatmate in class = 同桌

seaward
/ˈsiːwəd/
核心：形容词、副词：向海的、朝海的：the seaward side。也作 seawards（副词）。
① The seaward side of the island is wild and rocky, while the other side has quiet beaches.
= 那座岛朝海的一面荒凉多礁石，另一面则有安静的海滩。
the seaward side = 朝海的一面
seaward = 向海的
② The little boat drifted seaward when the old rope broke in the strong wind during the night.
= 夜里风大，旧绳子断了，小船向海里漂去。
drift seaward = 向海漂去

seawater
/ˈsiːwɔːtə(r)/
核心：名词，不可数：海水。
① You should not drink seawater, because the salt in it will make you even more thirsty.
= 你不该喝海水，因为里面的盐会让你更渴。
seawater = 海水
② The factory takes in seawater and turns it into fresh drinking water for the city.
= 那家工厂抽进海水，把它变成供全市饮用的淡水。
take in seawater = 抽进海水

seaway
/ˈsiːweɪ/
核心：名词，可数：（可通海轮的）内陆航道：the St Lawrence Seaway；海上航道。
① Large ships can reach the Great Lakes by sailing up the seaway from the Atlantic.
= 大型船只可以从大西洋沿着那条内陆航道上溯到达五大湖。
the seaway = 内陆航道
② The government spent billions widening the seaway so that bigger ships could use it.
= 政府花了几十亿拓宽那条航道，好让更大的船通行。
widen a seaway = 拓宽航道

seaweed
/ˈsiːwiːd/
核心：名词，不可数或可数：海藻、海草：长在海里的植物，有些可以吃。
① The beach was covered in seaweed after the storm, and it smelled terrible.
= 暴风雨过后，海滩上满是海藻，气味难闻极了。
seaweed = 海藻
② In Japan, many dishes are made with dried seaweed, such as the rolls of rice and fish.
= 在日本，许多菜都用干海苔做，比如那种米饭夹鱼的卷儿。
dried seaweed = 干海苔

seaworthiness
/ˈsiːwɜːðinəs/
核心：名词，不可数：（船只的）适航性：能否安全出海航行的状态。
① Inspectors checked the seaworthiness of the old ferry before it was allowed to carry passengers.
= 检查员先检查了那艘旧渡轮的适航性，才准它载客。
the seaworthiness of a ship = 船只的适航性
seaworthiness = 适航性
② The captain was blamed for not checking the seaworthiness of his boat before the storm.
= 船长因没有在暴风雨前检查船只适航性而受到指责。
check the seaworthiness of something = 检查某物的适航性

seaworthy
/ˈsiːwɜːði/
核心：形容词：（船只）适于航海的、经得起风浪的。
① The old fishing boat was no longer seaworthy, so they turned it into a garden bench.
= 那条旧渔船已经不能出海了，他们就把它改成了一张花园长椅。
seaworthy = 适于航海的
no longer seaworthy = 不再适航
② The engineers worked for weeks to make the damaged ship seaworthy again.
= 工程师们干了几个星期，才让那艘受损的船重新能出海。
make a ship seaworthy = 使船适航

sebaceous
/sɪˈbeɪʃəs/
核心：形容词（解剖）：皮脂的、分泌皮脂的：sebaceous glands（皮脂腺）。
① Spots appear when the sebaceous glands in the skin become blocked with oil.
= 皮肤里的皮脂腺被油脂堵住时，就会长痘痘。
sebaceous glands = 皮脂腺
sebaceous = 皮脂的
② The doctor removed a small sebaceous cyst from the back of his neck.
= 医生从他后颈切除了一个小皮脂腺囊肿。
a sebaceous cyst = 皮脂腺囊肿

sebum
/ˈsiːbəm/
核心：名词，不可数（医学）：皮脂：皮脂腺分泌、保护皮肤和头发的油脂。
① Teenagers often have oily skin and spots because their bodies produce more sebum.
= 青少年的皮肤常常很油、爱长痘，因为他们分泌的皮脂更多。
sebum = 皮脂
② Washing your face too often removes the natural sebum that protects your skin.
= 洗脸太勤会洗掉保护皮肤的天然皮脂。
the natural sebum = 天然皮脂

sec
/sek/
核心：名词，单数（口语）：一会儿、片刻：wait a sec、just a sec。形容词：（葡萄酒、香槟）干的、不甜的。
① Wait a sec, I can't find my keys, and I think I left them in the kitchen.
= 等一下，我找不到钥匙了，我想是落在厨房里了。
wait a sec = 等一下
a sec = 一会儿
② We ordered a bottle of demi-sec champagne, which was a little sweet, to go with the cake.
= 我们点了一瓶半干型香槟配蛋糕，有一点甜味。
demi-sec = 半干型的

secateurs
/ˌsekəˈtɜːz/
核心：名词，复数（英式）：修枝剪：园艺用的小剪刀。说一把要用 a pair of secateurs。
① My grandmother always carries a pair of secateurs in her pocket when she walks around the garden.
= 我祖母在花园里转悠时，口袋里总揣着一把修枝剪。
a pair of secateurs = 一把修枝剪
secateurs = 修枝剪
② Cut the dead flowers off the rose bush with sharp secateurs to help new ones grow.
= 用锋利的修枝剪把玫瑰丛上的枯花剪掉，有助于长出新花。
sharp secateurs = 锋利的修枝剪

secede
/sɪˈsiːd/
核心：动词，不及物（正式）：（地区、组织）脱离、退出（国家、联盟）：secede from something。名词 secession。
① The southern states tried to secede from the United States in 1861, and a civil war followed.
= 一八六一年南方各州试图脱离美国，随后爆发了内战。
secede from something = 脱离某国家或组织
secede = 脱离
② In the end, the people of the island voted to secede and become an independent country.
= 最终，那座岛上的人投票决定脱离出去，成为一个独立国家。
vote to secede = 投票脱离

secession
/sɪˈseʃn/
核心：名词，不可数或可数：（地区从国家、组织中的）脱离、分裂。
① The secession of the region led to years of war and thousands of deaths.
= 那个地区的分离导致了多年战争和成千上万人死亡。
the secession of something = 某地的脱离
secession = 脱离、分裂
② Many people in the north were strongly against secession and wanted the country to stay united.
= 北方许多人强烈反对分裂，希望国家保持统一。
be against secession = 反对分裂

secessionist
/sɪˈseʃənɪst/
核心：名词，可数：分离主义者、主张脱离的人。形容词：分离主义的。
① The secessionists want the three northern provinces to break away and become an independent country.
= 分离主义者希望北部三省分离出去，成为一个独立国家。
secessionists = 分离主义者
② The government refused to talk to the secessionist leaders until they stopped the violence.
= 政府拒绝与分离主义领导人对话，除非他们停止暴力。
secessionist leaders = 分离主义领导人

seclude
/sɪˈkluːd/
核心：动词，及物（正式）：使隔绝、使隐居：seclude yourself from something。形容词 secluded、名词 seclusion 另立条。
① After the scandal, the singer secluded herself in a house in the mountains and refused to see anyone.
= 丑闻过后，那位歌手隐居到山里的一栋房子里，谁都不见。
seclude yourself = 隐居、闭门不出
seclude = 使隔绝
② The writer secluded himself from his family for a year to finish his novel.
= 那位作家一年不和家人来往，闭门写完了他的小说。
seclude yourself from somebody = 与某人隔绝

secluded
/sɪˈkluːdɪd/
核心：形容词：（地方）僻静的、与世隔绝的：a secluded beach；（生活）隐居的。
① We found a secluded beach where there was nobody else, only birds and the sound of the waves.
= 我们找到一处僻静的海滩，那里一个人也没有，只有鸟和海浪声。
a secluded beach = 僻静的海滩
secluded = 僻静的
② After her husband died, she lived a secluded life in a small cottage in the hills.
= 丈夫去世后，她在山里的一座小屋里过着隐居的生活。
a secluded life = 隐居的生活

seclusion
/sɪˈkluːʒn/
核心：名词，不可数：隔绝、隐居；僻静：in seclusion。
① The old actor lived in seclusion for the last twenty years of his life.
= 那位老演员在人生的最后二十年过着隐居生活。
in seclusion = 隐居
seclusion = 隐居
② The hotel offers peace and seclusion, far away from the noise of the city.
= 那家酒店远离城市喧嚣，提供宁静与清幽。
peace and seclusion = 宁静与清幽

second
/ˈsekənd/（第二、秒）/sɪˈkɒnd/（借调）
核心：一、序数词、形容词、副词：第二（的）；第二位、第二名：come second；次于……的：second only to；副词 secondly 是第二、其次（列举时）。
二、名词，可数：秒；（口语）一会儿、片刻：in a second、a split second；（复数）（口语）第二份饭菜：have seconds；（复数）次品；（决斗、拳击中的）助手。
三、动词：附议、支持（提议）；（英式，读 /sɪˈkɒnd/）借调、暂调（到别的部门）：be seconded to。
习语：second to none 是首屈一指；on second thoughts 是转念一想；second nature 是第二天性、习以为常。
复合词：second best 是次好的；second class 是二等；second hand / second-hand 是二手的，second-hand smoke 是二手烟；second home 是第二住所；second in command 是副指挥；second language 是第二语言，second language acquisition 是二语习得；second lieutenant 是少尉；second name 是姓、中间名；second screen 是第二屏幕；second-degree 是二级的；second-generation 是第二代的；second-guess 是事后批评、猜测；second sight 是预见力；second string 是替补；second wind 是重新振作；second banana 是配角；second chamber 是（议会）第二院、上院。
① There are sixty seconds in a minute and sixty minutes in an hour.
= 一分钟有六十秒，一小时有六十分钟。
second = 秒
口语里 a second 常只是「一会儿」，和 a minute 一样不按字面计时。
② She finished second in the school race, just a few metres behind her older sister.
= 她在学校赛跑中得了第二名，只比她姐姐落后几米。
second = 第二
come second = 得第二名
③ I'll be with you in a second; I just need to finish this email.
= 我马上就来，只要把这封邮件写完。
in a second = 马上
④ It all happened in a split second, and nobody saw who threw the stone.
= 一切都发生在一瞬间，谁也没看见是谁扔的石头。
a split second = 一瞬间
⑤ The hotel's restaurant is second to none, and people come from miles away to eat there.
= 那家酒店的餐厅首屈一指，人们从老远的地方赶来吃饭。
second to none = 首屈一指
⑥ I was going to drive, but on second thoughts I will take the train because of the snow.
= 我本来要开车去，可转念一想，因为下雪还是坐火车吧。
on second thoughts = 转念一想
⑦ After years of practice, playing the piano has become second nature to her.
= 多年练习之后，弹钢琴对她来说已经习以为常。
second nature = 第二天性
⑧ Firstly, the house is too small; secondly, it is too far from the station.
= 第一，那房子太小；第二，离车站太远。
secondly = 第二、其次
⑨ My grandmother's dinner was so good that everybody at the table asked for seconds.
= 祖母做的晚饭太好吃了，桌上每个人都要了第二份。
have seconds = 再来一份
⑩ "I propose that we finish the meeting now and go home." "I'll second that, it's late."
= 「我提议现在就结束会议回家。」「我附议，太晚了。」
second a proposal = 附议提议
⑪ She was seconded to the London office for six months to help with the new project.
= 她被借调到伦敦办事处六个月，帮忙做新项目。
be seconded to = 被借调到
⑫ Living with my parents was second best, but it was all we could afford.
= 和父母住在一起不是最好的选择，可我们只负担得起这个。
second best = 次好的选择
⑬ We travelled second class because the first-class tickets were far too expensive.
= 我们坐的是二等座，因为头等座太贵了。
second class = 二等
⑭ I bought a second-hand bike for twenty pounds, and it still works perfectly.
= 我花二十英镑买了一辆二手自行车，到现在还好好的。
second-hand = 二手的
second hand = 二手的（不加连字符的写法）
⑮ Doctors warn parents that second-hand smoke in the car is dangerous for young children.
= 医生提醒家长，车里的二手烟对幼儿有害。
second-hand smoke = 二手烟
⑯ They have a second home by the sea where they spend every summer.
= 他们在海边有第二套房，每年夏天都去那儿住。
a second home = 第二住所
⑰ The captain was injured, so his second in command took control of the ship.
= 船长受了伤，所以副指挥接管了那艘船。
second in command = 副指挥、副手
⑱ English is her second language, but she speaks it better than most native speakers.
= 英语是她的第二语言，可她说得比大多数母语者还好。
a second language = 第二语言
⑲ At the university she studies second language acquisition and how adults learn new words.
= 她在大学里研究二语习得，以及成年人怎样学新词。
second language acquisition = 二语习得
⑳ My grandfather joined the army at eighteen and became a second lieutenant two years later.
= 我祖父十八岁参军，两年后当上了少尉。
a second lieutenant = 少尉
㉑ Please write your first name and your second name on the form.
= 请在表格上填写你的名字和姓。
second name = 姓；中间名
㉒ Many viewers use their phone as a second screen while they watch television.
= 许多观众一边看电视一边把手机当作第二屏幕。
a second screen = 第二屏幕
㉓ The child suffered second-degree burns when the hot water fell on her arm.
= 热水洒在那孩子胳膊上，造成了二度烧伤。
second-degree burns = 二度烧伤
㉔ He is a second-generation Italian whose grandparents came to London in the 1950s.
= 他是第二代意大利移民，祖父母二十世纪五十年代来到伦敦。
second-generation = 第二代的
㉕ It is easy to second-guess the manager after the match, but he had to decide in seconds.
= 赛后事后诸葛亮很容易，可经理当时得在几秒钟内做决定。
second-guess = 事后批评、事后猜测
㉖ My grandmother claimed to have second sight and always knew when someone was going to visit.
= 我祖母自称有预见力，总能知道谁要来拜访。
second sight = 预见力
㉗ The coach played his second string in the cup match to rest the best players.
= 教练在杯赛中派上替补阵容，让主力球员休息。
second string = 替补
㉘ I was tired after ten miles, but I got my second wind and finished the race.
= 跑了十英里我就累了，可后来又缓过劲来，跑完了全程。
get your second wind = 重新振作、缓过劲来
㉙ After twenty years, he was tired of being second banana to his famous brother on the show.
= 二十年过去了，他厌倦了在节目里给他那位名人哥哥当配角。
second banana = 配角
㉚ The bill must also be approved by the second chamber of parliament.
= 那项法案还必须经过议会第二院批准。
the second chamber = （议会）第二院

secondary
/ˈsekəndri/
核心：形容词：次要的、第二位的：of secondary importance；（教育）中等的、中学的：secondary school、secondary education；（医学）继发的；次生的、间接的。副词 secondarily 是次要地、间接地。
复合词：secondary education 是中等教育；secondary modern（英式旧时）是现代中学；secondary industry 是第二产业、制造业；secondary picketing 是（声援他厂的）间接纠察；secondary source 是二手资料；secondary stress（语音）是次重音。
① For him, money is of secondary importance; what matters is enjoying his work.
= 对他来说，钱是次要的，重要的是喜欢自己的工作。
of secondary importance = 次要的
secondary = 次要的
② Children in this country start secondary school at the age of eleven.
= 这个国家的孩子十一岁上中学。
secondary school = 中学
③ The new government wants to give every child in the country a good secondary education.
= 新政府希望让全国每个孩子都受到良好的中等教育。
secondary education = 中等教育
④ He went to a secondary modern in the 1960s and left school at fifteen.
= 他二十世纪六十年代上的是现代中学，十五岁就离开了学校。
a secondary modern = （英式旧时）现代中学
⑤ In our geography lesson we learned that factories that make cars belong to secondary industry.
= 地理课上我们学到，造汽车的工厂属于第二产业。
secondary industry = 第二产业
⑥ The law made secondary picketing illegal, so workers could only strike at their own factory.
= 那项法律禁止间接纠察，所以工人只能在本厂罢工。
secondary picketing = 间接纠察
⑦ A newspaper report about a battle is a secondary source, while a soldier's diary is a primary one.
= 关于一场战役的报纸报道是二手资料，而士兵的日记是一手资料。
a secondary source = 二手资料
⑧ In the word "dictionary", the main stress is on "dic" and the secondary stress is on "na".
= 在「dictionary」这个词里，主重音在「dic」上，次重音在「na」上。
secondary stress = 次重音
⑨ The doctor explained that the disease affects the lungs primarily and the heart only secondarily.
= 医生解释说，那种病主要影响肺部，对心脏的影响是次要的。
secondarily = 次要地

seconder
/ˈsekəndə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（提议的）附议人、支持者。
① The proposal needs a seconder before the committee can vote on it.
= 那项提议需要有人附议，委员会才能表决。
a seconder = 附议人
② She agreed to be my seconder when I stood for election to the club committee.
= 我参选俱乐部委员会时，她同意当我的附议人。
be somebody's seconder = 当某人的附议人

secondment
/sɪˈkɒndmənt/
核心：名词，可数或不可数（英式）：借调、暂调：on secondment to somewhere。
① The teacher is on secondment to the education department for a year.
= 那位老师被借调到教育部门一年。
on secondment = 在借调中
secondment = 借调
② The company offers its young engineers secondments to its offices abroad for a year or two.
= 那家公司给年轻工程师提供借调到海外分公司一两年的机会。
a secondment abroad = 海外借调

secrecy
/ˈsiːkrəsi/
核心：名词，不可数：保密、秘密状态：in secrecy、shrouded in secrecy、swear somebody to secrecy。
① The plans for the new factory were made in great secrecy, and even the workers did not know.
= 新工厂的计划是在高度保密的情况下制订的，连工人都不知道。
in secrecy = 秘密地
secrecy = 保密
② She swore her sister to secrecy before telling her about the new job.
= 她先让妹妹发誓保密，然后才把新工作的事告诉她。
swear somebody to secrecy = 让某人发誓保密

secret
/ˈsiːkrət/
核心：名词，可数：秘密：keep a secret、tell somebody a secret；（单数）秘诀、诀窍：the secret of success；奥秘：the secrets of nature。形容词：秘密的、保密的：a secret meeting；（人）不公开的、暗中的：a secret admirer。副词 secretly 是秘密地、偷偷地。名词 secrecy 另立条。
习语：in secret 是秘密地；an open secret 是公开的秘密。
复合词：secret agent 是特工、密探；secret police 是秘密警察；secret service 是特工机构、情报机关。
① Can you keep a secret? I am going to ask her to marry me tonight.
= 你能保密吗？我今晚要向她求婚。
keep a secret = 保密
② The two countries held secret talks for months before they announced the agreement.
= 两国在宣布那项协议之前，秘密谈判了好几个月。
secret talks = 秘密谈判
secret = 秘密的
③ The secret of her success is hard work and a lot of patience.
= 她成功的秘诀是勤奋和极大的耐心。
the secret of = ……的秘诀
④ The group met in secret in a cellar under the old church.
= 那个团体在老教堂下面的一个地窖里秘密集会。
in secret = 秘密地
⑤ It is an open secret that the minister is going to resign after the summer.
= 部长夏天过后要辞职，这已是公开的秘密。
an open secret = 公开的秘密
⑥ He secretly saved money for years so that he could buy his wife a ring.
= 他偷偷攒了好几年钱，好给妻子买一枚戒指。
secretly = 偷偷地
⑦ In the film, the quiet bank clerk is really a secret agent working for the government.
= 那部电影里，那个沉默寡言的银行职员其实是为政府工作的特工。
a secret agent = 特工
⑧ People were afraid to speak freely because the secret police were everywhere.
= 人们不敢自由说话，因为到处都有秘密警察。
the secret police = 秘密警察
⑨ He worked for the secret service for thirty years, but his family never knew what he did.
= 他在情报机关工作了三十年，可家里人从不知道他是干什么的。
the secret service = 特工机构、情报机关

secretarial
/ˌsekrəˈteəriəl/
核心：形容词：秘书的、文秘的：secretarial work、a secretarial course。
① She did a secretarial course after school and got a job in a lawyer's office.
= 她中学毕业后上了一个文秘课程，在一家律师事务所找到了工作。
a secretarial course = 文秘课程
secretarial = 秘书的
② The office needs someone to help with typing, filing and other secretarial work.
= 办公室需要一个人帮忙打字、归档和做其他文秘工作。
secretarial work = 文秘工作

secretariat
/ˌsekrəˈteəriət/
核心：名词，可数（常作 the secretariat）：（国际组织、政党的）秘书处、书记处。
① The United Nations secretariat is based in a tall glass building in New York.
= 联合国秘书处设在纽约的一栋高大的玻璃大楼里。
the secretariat = 秘书处
② The party's secretariat sent a letter to every member about the new rules.
= 党的书记处给每个党员发了一封关于新规定的信。
the party's secretariat = 党的书记处

secretary
/ˈsekrətri/
核心：名词，可数：秘书：处理信件、电话、日程的职员；（俱乐部、协会的）干事、秘书；（英国）大臣：the Foreign Secretary；（美国）部长：the Secretary of State。复合词 secretary general 是（国际组织的）秘书长；secretary bird 是蛇鹫（一种非洲猛禽）。
① My secretary will send you the details of the meeting by email this afternoon.
= 我的秘书今天下午会把会议详情用电子邮件发给你。
secretary = 秘书
② The Foreign Secretary flew to Washington to discuss the crisis with the American president.
= 外交大臣飞往华盛顿，与美国总统商讨这场危机。
secretary = 大臣
③ My father has been the secretary of the village cricket club for twenty years.
= 我父亲当村板球俱乐部的干事已经二十年了。
the secretary of a club = 俱乐部干事
④ The Secretary General of the United Nations called for an end to the fighting.
= 联合国秘书长呼吁停止战斗。
the Secretary General = 秘书长
⑤ We watched a secretary bird walking through the long grass, looking for snakes.
= 我们看到一只蛇鹫在高高的草丛里走着找蛇。
a secretary bird = 蛇鹫

secrete
/sɪˈkriːt/
核心：动词，及物：（生物）分泌：secrete a hormone；（正式）藏匿、隐藏：secrete something in somewhere。名词 secretion。
① The glands in your mouth secrete saliva when you smell good food.
= 闻到好吃的东西时，嘴里的腺体会分泌唾液。
secrete = 分泌
secrete saliva = 分泌唾液
② The thief had secreted the jewels inside a hollow book on the shelf.
= 那个小偷把珠宝藏在了架子上一本挖空的书里。
secrete something = 藏匿某物

secretion
/sɪˈkriːʃn/

① The doctor explained that the new medicine reduces the secretion of acid in the stomach.
= 医生解释说，那种新药能减少胃酸分泌。
the secretion of something = 某物的分泌
secretion = 分泌
② Some brightly coloured frogs produce poisonous secretions from their skin to protect themselves.
= 有些颜色鲜艳的青蛙从皮肤分泌有毒物质来保护自己。
secretions = 分泌物

secretive
/ˈsiːkrətɪv/
核心：形容词：（人）惯于保密的、遮遮掩掩的：be secretive about something。副词 secretively 是遮遮掩掩地。名词 secretiveness 另立条。
① He has always been very secretive about his work, so we have no idea how much he earns.
= 他对自己的工作一向遮遮掩掩，所以我们根本不知道他挣多少钱。
be secretive about something = 对某事遮遮掩掩
secretive = 惯于保密的
② The two girls whispered secretively in the corner and laughed whenever anyone came near.
= 那两个女孩在角落里偷偷地说悄悄话，谁一走近她们就笑。
secretively = 遮遮掩掩地

secretiveness
/ˈsiːkrətɪvnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：遮遮掩掩、守口如瓶的习性。
① His secretiveness about money made his wife suspicious, so she checked the bank statements.
= 他在钱的事上遮遮掩掩，引起了妻子的怀疑，于是她查了银行对账单。
somebody's secretiveness = 某人的遮遮掩掩
secretiveness = 遮遮掩掩
② The secretiveness of the company made journalists even more curious about its plans.
= 那家公司的守口如瓶让记者们对它的计划更加好奇。
the secretiveness of something = 某机构的守口如瓶

sect
/sekt/

/sekˈteəriən/

/sekˈteəriənɪzəm/

section
/ˈsekʃn/
核心：名词，可数：部分、段：a section of the road；（文章、书的）章节、节：section by section；（机构的）部门、科；（乐团的）声部：the string section；（报纸的）版面：the sports section；剖面、截面：cross section。动词：把……分成段；（医学）切开；section off 是把……隔开。
① The second section of the book explains how the city grew in the nineteenth century.
= 那本书的第二章讲那座城市在十九世纪是如何发展起来的。

② We read the contract section by section to make sure we understood everything.
= 我们逐节读了合同，确保每处都弄懂了。

③ The drawing in the biology book shows a cross section of the human heart.
= 生物书里那幅图展示的是人类心脏的横截面。

④ The survey asked a cross section of people from all over the country.
= 那项调查询问了来自全国各地的有代表性的人群。

⑤ A long section of the motorway near the airport is closed this weekend for repairs.
= 这个周末，机场附近高速公路有很长一段封闭维修。
a section of something = 某物的一段
⑥ She plays the violin in the string section of the city orchestra.
= 她在市交响乐团弦乐声部拉小提琴。
the string section = 弦乐声部
⑦ At breakfast my father always reads the sports section of the newspaper first.
= 吃早饭时，我父亲看报纸总是先看体育版。
the sports section = 体育版
⑧ The police sectioned off the area around the house with red and white tape.

sectional
/ˈsekʃənl/

sector
/ˈsektə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（经济的）部门、领域、行业：the public / private sector、the service sector；（城市、地区的）区域、地带；（数学）扇形。形容词 sectoral 另立条。
① She left her job in the private sector to work for a hospital run by the government.
= 她辞去了私营部门的工作，去一家政府办的医院上班。

② Jobs in the public sector are usually safer but less well paid.
= 公共部门的工作通常更稳定，但工资较低。
the public sector = 公共部门
③ The army divided the city into four sectors, each controlled by a different country.
= 军队把那座城市分成四个区，各由一个国家控制。

④ Cut the circle into eight equal sectors, like slices of a cake.
= 把那个圆分成八个相等的扇形，像切蛋糕一样。
a sector = 扇形

/ˈsektərəl/

secular
/ˈsekjələ(r)/

= 法国是世俗国家，所以公立学校不教宗教。

② The village choir sings both religious and secular music at its Christmas concerts.

secularism
/ˈsekjələrɪzəm/

= 新宪法以世俗主义和各宗教信徒权利平等为基础。

② The debate about secularism in schools has divided the country for years.

secularism in schools = 学校里的世俗主义

secularist
/ˈsekjələrɪst/

① The secularists argued that the state should not pay for religious schools.
= 世俗主义者主张，国家不应该为宗教学校出钱。

② She belongs to a secularist group that campaigns against prayers in parliament.

/ˌsekjələraɪˈzeɪʃn/

= 那本书描述了二十世纪欧洲社会的世俗化。

② Many old churches have been turned into flats as a result of secularization.
= 由于世俗化，许多老教堂已经改成了公寓。

secularize
/ˈsekjələraɪz/

① The new government secularized the schools and stopped paying the churches to run them.
= 新政府使学校世俗化，不再出钱让教会办学。

② As the country became richer after the war, it also became much more secularized.
= 战后随着国家越来越富裕，它也变得世俗化得多。

secure
/sɪˈkjʊə(r)/
核心：形容词：安全的、牢固的：a secure lock；可靠的、有保障的：a secure job；（心理上）有安全感的、自信的：feel secure；（地方）戒备森严的。反义是 insecure（不安全的、没安全感的）。动词：（正式）获得、争取到：secure a loan、secure a place；固定、系牢：secure something to something；保护、使安全。副词 securely 是牢固地、安全地。名词 security 另立条。复合词 secure training centre（英式）是少年犯教养中心。
① Make sure the ladder is secure before you climb up to the roof.
= 爬上屋顶之前，一定要确认梯子放稳了。
secure = 牢固的
② After years of temporary jobs, she finally has a secure job with a good pension.
= 打了多年临时工之后，她终于有了一份有保障、退休金不错的工作。
a secure job = 有保障的工作
③ Young children need to feel secure and loved at home before they can do well at school.
= 幼儿在家里需要先感到安全、被爱，才能在学校表现好。
feel secure = 有安全感
反义：
insecure = 不安全的、没安全感的
④ The company secured a loan from the bank to build a new factory.
= 那家公司从银行获得了一笔贷款，用来建新工厂。
secure = 获得
secure a loan = 获得贷款
⑤ Our team secured a place in the final with a goal in the last minute.
= 我们队在最后一分钟进了一球，锁定了决赛席位。
secure a place = 锁定席位
⑥ The sailors secured the boat to the post with a thick rope.
= 水手们用一根粗绳把船系牢在桩子上。
secure something to something = 把某物系牢在某处
⑦ When you go out to the beach, keep your passport securely locked in the hotel safe.
= 你出去海滩玩的时候，把护照牢牢锁在酒店的保险箱里。
securely = 牢固地、安全地
⑧ The young offender was sent to a secure training centre for six months.
= 那名少年犯被送进少年犯教养中心六个月。
a secure training centre = （英式）少年犯教养中心

security
/sɪˈkjʊərəti/
核心：名词：安全、保安（不可数）：national security、airport security；（单数）安全感：a sense of security；保障（不可数）：job security；（单数）（机场、公司的）安检、保安部门：go through security；（贷款的）抵押品（不可数）；（复数 securities）证券。
复合词：security forces 是（军警等）安全部队；security blanket 是（孩子离不开的）安心毯，比喻带来安全感的东西；security deposit 是押金；security hole 是（电脑系统的）安全漏洞。
① Security at the airport has been increased after last week's bomb threat.
= 上周的炸弹威胁之后，机场加强了安保。
security = 安全
② Having a regular income every month gives you a sense of security about the future.
= 每个月有稳定的收入，会让你对未来有一种安全感。
a sense of security = 安全感
③ Many workers worry about job security now that the factory has a new owner.
= 工厂换了新老板，许多工人担心工作保障问题。
job security = 工作保障
④ It took us forty minutes to get through security at the airport.
= 我们在机场花了四十分钟才通过安检。
go through security = 通过安检
⑤ The bank asked for his house as security for the loan to start his business.
= 银行要求他用房子作创业贷款的抵押。
as security for a loan = 作贷款的抵押
⑥ The security forces closed all the roads into the city after the attack.
= 袭击发生后，安全部队封锁了所有进城的道路。
the security forces = 安全部队
⑦ My little brother still carries his old security blanket everywhere he goes.
= 我小弟弟走到哪儿都还抱着他那条旧安心毯。
a security blanket = 安心毯
⑧ We had to pay a security deposit of one month's rent before we moved into the flat.
= 搬进那套公寓之前，我们得付相当于一个月房租的押金。
a security deposit = 押金
⑨ The company fixed a security hole that allowed hackers to steal customers' passwords.
= 那家公司修补了一个让黑客能窃取顾客密码的安全漏洞。
a security hole = 安全漏洞

sedan
/sɪˈdæn/

sedate
/sɪˈdeɪt/
核心：形容词：（人、步伐）稳重的、沉静的；（节奏）缓慢的、不慌不忙的。动词：给……服镇静剂：be sedated。副词 sedately 是稳重地、缓慢地。名词 sedation 另立条。
① We walked at a sedate pace along the river, stopping often to look at the boats.
= 我们沿着河边不紧不慢地走，常常停下来看船。

② The patient was so frightened that the doctors had to sedate him before the operation.
= 那个病人太害怕了，医生只好在手术前给他用了镇静剂。

③ The old car moved sedately down the country lane at twenty miles an hour.
= 那辆老车以每小时二十英里的速度慢悠悠地沿着乡间小路开着。
sedately = 缓慢地

sedation
/sɪˈdeɪʃn/

① The patient was kept under sedation for two days after the operation.
= 手术后，病人在镇静状态下维持了两天。
under sedation = 处于镇静状态
sedation = 镇静
② The dentist offered him sedation because he was so nervous about having the tooth out.
= 他对拔牙非常紧张，牙医就给他用了镇静。
offer sedation = 用镇静

sedative
/ˈsedətɪv/

① The doctor gave her a mild sedative to help her sleep after the accident.
= 事故后，医生给了她一点温和的镇静剂帮她入睡。

② Some herbal teas have a sedative effect and help people relax in the evening.
= 有些草药茶有镇静作用，能帮助人们晚上放松。

sedentary

Seder
/ˈseɪdə(r)/

① Every spring, the whole family gathers at my grandmother's house for the Seder.

sedge
/sedʒ/

= 湖边长着高高的莎草，鸭子躲在草茎之间。
